Beyond algorithmic contributions, we provide an analysis of RL learning dynamics and identify an empirical upper bound on Pass@k, shedding light on intrinsic exploration limits in RL-based reasoning models. Our findings suggest that alternating between RL and IRL offers an effective pathway toward extending the exploration capacity of reasoning-oriented large language models.

</details>


### [40] [BIASEDTALES-ML: A Multilingual Dataset for Analyzing Narrative Attribute Distributions in LLM-Generated Stories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17008)
*Yuxuan Ouyang,yingfeng luo,JingBo Zhu,Tong Xiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了BiasedTales-ML，一个包含约35万儿童故事的大规模平行语料库，涵盖8种语言，用于研究多语言叙事生成中的跨语言偏差和模式差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI安全和对齐评估主要关注英语，而跨语言对齐行为的泛化研究不足。儿童故事在社会文化学习中扮演重要角色，需要研究多语言叙事生成的跨语言模式差异。

Method: 1) 创建BiasedTales-ML语料库：使用全排列提示设计在8种类型和文化多样的语言中生成约35万儿童故事；2) 提出结构化生成器-提取器管道；3) 采用多维度分布分析框架，分析叙事属性在不同语言、模型和社会条件下的变化。

Result: 研究发现：1) 叙事生成模式存在显著的跨语言变异性，英语中观察到的分布特征在其他语言中并不总是相似，特别是在低资源语言中；2) 在叙事层面识别出涉及角色、场景和主题重点的重复结构模式，这些模式在不同语言环境中表现不同。

Conclusion: 英语中心评估在多语言环境中描述社会基础叙事生成存在局限性。研究发布了数据集、代码和交互式可视化工具，支持未来多语言叙事分析和评估研究。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used to generate narrative content, including children's stories, which play an important role in social and cultural learning. Despite growing interest in AI safety and alignment, most existing evaluations focus primarily on English, leaving the cross-lingual generalization of aligned behavior underexplored. In this work, we introduce BiasedTales-ML, a large-scale parallel corpus of approximately 350,000 children's stories generated across eight typologically and culturally diverse languages using a full-permutation prompting design. We propose a structured generator-extractor pipeline and a multi-dimensional distributional analysis framework to examine how narrative attributes vary across languages, models, and social conditions. Our analysis reveals substantial cross-lingual variability in narrative generation patterns, indicating that distributions observed in English do not always exhibit similar characteristics in other languages, particularly in lower-resource settings. At the narrative level, we identify recurring structural patterns involving character roles, settings, and thematic emphasis, which manifest differently across linguistic contexts. These findings highlight the limitations of English-centric evaluation for characterizing socially grounded narrative generation in multilingual settings. We release the dataset, code, and an interactive visualization tool to support future research on multilingual narrative analysis and evaluation.

</details>


### [41] [Improving LLM Code Reasoning via Semantic Equivalence Self-Play with Formal Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17010)
*Antonio Valerio Miceli Barone,Poon Tsz Nok*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出基于自我博弈的Haskell语义等价框架，利用形式验证指导生成器和评估器的对抗训练，通过难度感知课程组织，并发布包含约28k验证程序的OpInstruct-HSx数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Haskell程序中语义等价性验证的挑战，通过结合形式验证和对抗训练来提升模型在语义等价任务上的性能。

Method: 采用自我博弈框架，生成器和评估器进行对抗训练，使用Liquid Haskell证明验证等价性，基于执行的对抗示例验证不等价性，通过难度感知课程组织训练过程。

Result: 评估器在下游任务中表现优异，在EquiBench上获得最高13.3个百分点的准确率提升，在PySecDB上也有持续改进。消融研究表明等价证明对模型推理能力至关重要。

Conclusion: 提出的自我博弈框架有效提升了语义等价验证能力，等价证明是模型获得推理能力的关键，同时发布了完整的训练流程和数据集。

Abstract: We introduce a self-play framework for semantic equivalence in Haskell, utilizing formal verification to guide adversarial training between a generator and an evaluator. The framework leverages Liquid Haskell proofs for validating equivalence and execution-based counterexamples for inequivalence, organized via a difficulty-aware curriculum. To facilitate this, we release \textbf{OpInstruct-HSx}, a synthetic dataset of $\approx$28k validated Haskell programs. Empirical experiments show that our evaluator transfers effectively to downstream tasks, achieving up to 13.3pp accuracy gain on EquiBench and consistent gains on PySecDB. Ablation studies on the SEQ-SINQ regimes indicate that while inequivalence supervision provides data volume, equivalence proofs are uniquely responsible for the model's reasoning capabilities. The entire training pipeline and dataset are publicly released on GitHub and Hugging Face respectively.

</details>


### [42] [Beyond Black-Box Labels: Interpretable Criteria for Diagnosing SubjectiveNLP Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17022)
*Nisrine Rair,Alban Goupil,Valeriu Vrabie,Emmanuel Chochoy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种模式级诊断方法，用于在确定黄金标签前审计专家设计的标注模式，通过分析多标注者的标准判断来区分两种失败模式：标准不稳定和类别重叠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统主观NLP数据集通常将标注者判断聚合成单一黄金标签，难以诊断分歧是由于标准不清晰、类别界限模糊还是合理的多元性造成的。

Method: 提出模式级诊断方法，使用多标注者的标准判断（而非最终标签）来审计标注模式。该方法分离两种失败模式：1）标准不稳定（难以操作化的边界），2）系统性重叠（互斥类别间边界模糊）。

Result: 应用于商业文档的说服价值提取任务时发现：分歧并非分散的，不稳定性集中在少数标准中，近一半覆盖的句子激活了多个类别。这些信号与领域专家的分歧点一致。

Conclusion: 该方法提供基于证据的审计工具，可用于收紧标注指南、修订类别结构或重新考虑标注范式，有助于在黄金标签确定前改进标注质量。

Abstract: Subjective NLP datasets typically aggregate annotator judgments into a single gold label, making it difficult to diagnose whether disagreement reflects unclear criteria, collapsed distinctions, or legitimate plurality. We propose a \emph{schema-level diagnostic} for auditing expert-designed annotation schemas \emph{prior to} gold-label commitment, using only multi-annotator criterion judgments. The diagnostic separates two failure modes: unstable criteria with hard-to-operationalize boundaries, and systematic overlap that blurs the boundaries between mutually exclusive categories. Applied to persuasive value extraction in commercial documents, we find that disagreement is not diffuse: instability concentrates in a few criteria, while nearly half of covered sentences activate multiple categories. These signals align with where domain experts disagree, yielding an evidence-based audit for tightening guidelines, revising category structure, or reconsidering the annotation paradigm.

</details>


### [43] [Where is the Mind? Persona Vectors and LLM Individuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17031)
*Pierre Beckmann,Patrick Butlin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文探讨大型语言模型的个体化问题，即哪些相关实体应被视为心智。通过机制可解释性方法，结合人格向量、人格空间和涌现错位等实证研究，提出了三种主要观点：虚拟实例观、虚拟实例-人格观和模型-人格观。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本文旨在解决大型语言模型的个体化问题，即确定与这些模型相关的哪些实体（如果有的话）应该被识别为心智。这个问题对于理解人工智能系统的认知状态和道德地位具有重要意义。

Method: 采用机制可解释性方法，特别关注近期关于人格向量、人格空间和涌现错位的实证研究。通过分析注意力流在token时间中维持的准心理连接，以及人格文献中关于LLM内部结构的三种假设来构建理论框架。

Result: 提出了三种主要观点：1）虚拟实例观：基于注意力流维持跨token时间的准心理连接；2）虚拟实例-人格观：结合实例和人格特征的新观点；3）模型-人格观：将人格特征与模型整体关联的观点。后两种基于人格的观点被认为是很有前景的替代方案。

Conclusion: 大型语言模型的个体化问题可以通过机制可解释性方法得到有意义的探讨。虚拟实例观基于注意力机制的特性，而两种新的人格观则提供了有前景的替代视角，有助于更全面地理解LLM中可能存在的认知实体。

Abstract: The individuation problem for large language models asks which entities associated with them, if any, should be identified as minds. We approach this problem through mechanistic interpretability, engaging in particular with recent empirical work on persona vectors, persona space, and emergent misalignment. We argue that three views are the strongest candidates: the virtual instance view and two new views we introduce, the (virtual) instance-persona view and the model-persona view. First, we argue for the virtual instance view on the grounds that attention streams sustain quasi-psychological connections across token-time. Then we present the persona literature, organised around three hypotheses about the internal structure underlying personas in LLMs, and show that the two persona-based views are promising alternatives.

</details>


### [44] [Dynamic Emotion and Personality Profiling for Multimodal Deception Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17037)
*Li Zheng,Yanyi Luo,Hao Fei,Yuzhe Ding,Yujie Huang,Fei Li,Chong Teng,Donghong Ji*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Rel-DDEP框架，通过多模态多提示标注方案建立DDEP数据集，采用可靠性加权融合实现欺骗、情感和人格的联合检测，显著提升各项任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有欺骗检测方法缺乏样本级别的动态情感和人格标注，而人格因素和情感线索在欺骗检测中具有重要作用。需要建立包含动态标注的数据集并开发有效的联合检测框架。

Method: 1) 提出多模态多提示标注方案和严格标签质量评估标准，建立DDEP数据集；2) 提出Rel-DDEP框架，通过将模态特征映射到高维高斯分布空间量化不确定性；3) 进行可靠性加权融合，并包含对齐模块和排序约束模块实现联合检测。

Result: 在MDPE和DDEP数据集上的实验表明，Rel-DDEP显著优于现有最先进基线模型：欺骗检测F1分数提升2.53%，情感检测提升2.66%，人格检测提升9.30%。

Conclusion: 实验验证了为每个样本标注动态情感和人格标签的必要性，以及可靠性加权融合的有效性。该方法为信息安全和舆情分析提供了更准确的欺骗检测工具。

Abstract: Deception detection is of great significance for ensuring information security and conducting public opinion analysis, with personality factors and emotion cues playing a critical role. However, existing methods lack sample-level dynamic annotations for emotions and personality.In this paper, we propose an innovative multi-model multi-prompt annotation scheme and a strict label quality evaluation standard, and establish a multimodal joint detection dataset DDEP for deception, emotion, and personality. Meanwhile, we propose Rel-DDEP, an adaptive reliability-weighted fusion framework. Our framework quantifies uncertainty by mapping modal features to a high-dimensional Gaussian distribution space. It then performs reliability-weighted fusion and incorporates an alignment module and a sorting constraint module to achieve joint detection of deception, emotion, and personality. Experimental results on the MDPE and DDEP datasets show that our Rel-DDEP significantly outperforms the existing state-of-the-art baseline models in three tasks. The F1 score of the deception detection increases by 2.53%, that of the emotion detection increases by 2.66%, and that of the personality detection increases by 9.30%. The experiments fully verify the necessity of annotating dynamic emotion and personality labels for each sample and the effectiveness of reliability-weighted fusion.

</details>


### [45] [Efficient Task Adaptation in Large Language Models via Selective Parameter Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17051)
*Weijie Wan,Jiangjiang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种参数重要性评估方法，将LLM参数分为核心参数和非核心参数，在微调时固定核心参数只微调非核心参数，以缓解灾难性遗忘问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统微调策略在整个参数空间上进行训练，忽略了参数的异质性。一些参数对通用任务极其重要，而其他参数对特定任务更敏感。当为特定领域任务微调时，预训练阶段积累的通用知识往往被部分覆盖或遗忘，严重限制了LLM的泛化能力和可迁移性。

Method: 创新性地提出参数元素重要性评估方法，通过区分参数对通用语言能力任务和特定领域任务的重要性，将参数分为"核心参数"和"非核心参数"。在微调过程中固定核心参数，仅微调非核心参数。

Result: 在科学、医学和物理任务上使用GPT-J和LLaMA-3进行大量实验，结果表明该方法能够在增强模型适应性的同时缓解灾难性遗忘。

Conclusion: 提出的参数重要性评估和选择性微调方法有效解决了LLM在领域特定微调中的灾难性遗忘问题，平衡了通用知识和特定领域知识的保留。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated excellent performance in general language understanding, generation and other tasks. However, when fine-tuning for specific domain tasks, the general knowledge accumulated in the pre-training phase is often partially overwritten or forgotten due to parameter updates, which severely limits the generalization ability and transferability of LLMs. Traditional fine-tuning strategies mostly train on the entire parameter space, ignoring the heterogeneity of model parameters, that is, some parameters are extremely important for general tasks, while other parameters are more sensitive to specific tasks. To alleviate the above problems, this paper innovatively proposes a parameter element importance evaluation method, which divides parameters into "core parameters" and "non-core parameters" by distinguishing the importance of parameters for general language ability tasks and specific domain tasks, and fixes the core parameters during fine-tuning, and only fine-tunes the non-core parameters. Extensive experiments on scientific, medical and physical tasks using GPT-J and LLaMA-3 show that our method can mitigate catastrophic forgetting while enhancing the adaptability of the model.

</details>


### [46] [Jailbreaking Large Language Models with Morality Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17053)
*Ying Su,Mingen Zheng,Weili Diao,Haoran Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出利用越狱攻击来研究大语言模型内部多元价值观，构建了包含10.3K实例的道德数据集，设计了四种对抗攻击方法，发现LLMs和防护模型在道德感知攻击下存在严重漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多元对齐AI的目标是创建能够与道德多元化人类共存并服务的AI。虽然已有研究致力于增强LLMs的多元主义学习，但LLMs在多元价值观下产生道德内容的鲁棒性仍需探索。受越狱提示惊人说服能力的启发，研究者希望通过越狱攻击来研究LLMs内部的多元价值观。

Method: 1. 构建包含10.3K实例的道德数据集，分为"价值模糊"和"价值冲突"两类；2. 基于该数据集形式化四种对抗攻击方法，以操纵LLMs对道德问题的判断；3. 评估大语言模型和生成系统中常用的防护模型对这些攻击的脆弱性。

Result: 实验结果表明，LLMs和防护模型对这些微妙而复杂的道德感知攻击存在严重脆弱性，揭示了当前模型在多元价值观处理上的关键漏洞。

Conclusion: 研究通过越狱攻击揭示了LLMs和防护模型在多元价值观处理上的严重脆弱性，为多元对齐AI的安全性研究提供了重要洞见，强调了需要更鲁棒的道德防护机制。

Abstract: Pluralism alignment with AI has the sophisticated and necessary goal of creating AI that can coexist with and serve morally multifaceted humanity. Research towards pluralism alignment has many efforts in enhancing the learning of large language models (LLMs) to accomplish pluralism. Although this is essential, the robustness of LLMs to produce moral content over pluralistic values is still under exploration.Inspired by the astonishing persuasion abilities via jailbreak prompts, we propose to leverage jailbreak attacks to study LLMs' internal pluralistic values. In detail, we develop a morality dataset with 10.3K instances in two categories: Value Ambiguity and Value Conflict. We further formalize four adversarial attacks with the constructed dataset, to manipulate LLMs' judgment over the morality questions. We evaluate both the large language models and guardrail models which are typically used in generative systems with flexible user input. Our experiment results show that there is a critical vulnerability of LLMs and guardrail models to these subtle and sophisticated moral-aware attacks.

</details>


### [47] [Stability-Weighted Decoding for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17068)
*Yue Wu,Jian Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Stability-Weighted Decoding (SWD)方法，通过量化token的时间不稳定性来改进扩散大语言模型的解码策略，提高生成准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散大语言模型的解码策略依赖单步去噪计算的静态置信度指标，忽略了时间历史信息，容易导致不稳定token的过早解掩码，影响生成质量。

Method: 提出Stability-Weighted Decoding (SWD)策略，基于理论分析发现token的时间不稳定性（通过连续预测分布的KL散度量化）为其与剩余掩码上下文互信息的严格下界，将时间稳定性纳入token评分，作为任意基于分数解码策略的通用调制器。

Result: 在代码生成和数学推理基准测试中，SWD在不同评分指标和选择策略下都能一致提高生成准确性，并在不同加速比下保持显著性能优势，表现出优异的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SWD是一种无需训练、即插即用的解码策略，通过考虑token的时间稳定性来改进扩散大语言模型的解码过程，显著提升生成质量。

Abstract: Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) enable parallel text generation by iteratively denoising a fully masked sequence, unmasking a subset of masked tokens at each step. Existing decoding strategies rely on static confidence metrics computed at a single denoising step, ignoring temporal history and often leading to premature unmasking of unstable tokens. In this work, we theoretically establish that a token's temporal instability, quantified by the KL divergence between consecutive prediction distributions, provides a strict lower bound on its mutual information with the remaining masked context, indicating that temporally unstable tokens are inherently unsafe to unmask. Based on this insight, we propose Stability-Weighted Decoding (SWD), a training-free, plug-and-play strategy that incorporates temporal stability into token scoring and acts as a universal modulator for arbitrary score-based decoding policies. Experiments on code generation and mathematical reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that SWD consistently improves generation accuracy across representative scoring metrics and selection policies, and exhibits exceptional robustness, maintaining a significant performance lead over standard baselines across varying acceleration ratios.

</details>


### [48] [Abstain-R1: Calibrated Abstention and Post-Refusal Clarification via Verifiable RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17073)
*Skylar Zhai,Jingcheng Liang,Dongyeop Kang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种新的强化学习奖励机制RLVR，用于训练语言模型在面对无法回答的查询时，不仅能够主动拒绝回答，还能解释缺失的关键信息，而不是简单地猜测或产生幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的强化微调方法虽然提升了语言模型的推理能力，但会导致模型在面对无法回答的查询时进行猜测或产生幻觉。现有的拒绝回答方法要么训练模型产生通用的拒绝回应，要么鼓励后续澄清但无法验证这些澄清是否真正识别了关键缺失信息。

Method: 提出了clarification-aware RLVR奖励机制，该机制在可回答查询上奖励正确答案的同时，联合优化对不可回答查询的明确拒绝和语义对齐的拒绝后澄清。使用该奖励训练了Abstain-R1模型（3B参数）。

Result: 在Abstain-Test、Abstain-QA和SelfAware等基准测试中，Abstain-R1相比基础模型在不可回答查询的拒绝和澄清方面有显著提升，同时在可回答查询上保持强大性能，其表现与包括DeepSeek-R1在内的更大系统具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 校准的拒绝和澄清行为可以通过可验证的奖励机制学习获得，而不仅仅是随着模型规模扩大而自然出现。这表明通过精心设计的强化学习奖励，可以在相对较小的模型中实现可靠的拒绝回答和澄清能力。

Abstract: Reinforcement fine-tuning improves the reasoning ability of large language models, but it can also encourage them to answer unanswerable queries by guessing or hallucinating missing information. Existing abstention methods either train models to produce generic refusals or encourage follow-up clarifications without verifying whether those clarifications identify the key missing information. We study queries that are clear in meaning but cannot be reliably resolved from the given information, and argue that a reliable model should not only abstain, but also explain what is missing. We propose a clarification-aware RLVR reward that, while rewarding correct answers on answerable queries, jointly optimizes explicit abstention and semantically aligned post-refusal clarification on unanswerable queries. Using this reward, we train Abstain-R1, a 3B model that improves abstention and clarification on unanswerable queries while preserving strong performance on answerable ones. Experiments on Abstain-Test, Abstain-QA, and SelfAware show that Abstain-R1 substantially improves over its base model and achieves unanswerable-query behavior competitive with larger systems including DeepSeek-R1, suggesting that calibrated abstention and clarification can be learned through verifiable rewards rather than emerging from scale alone.

</details>


### [49] [Auditing Support Strategies in LLMs through Grounded Multi-Turn Social Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17079)
*Michelle Star,Andrew Aquilina,Yu-Ru Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出了一个多轮模拟框架来评估支持性语言模型，通过将Reddit社区中的求助叙述分解为有序片段逐轮呈现给模型，使用社会支持行为编码分析响应，并探究支持选择与模型内部对用户困扰感知的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对支持性语言模型的评估大多基于单轮、完全指定的提示，而现实中用户向聊天机器人寻求社会支持时会逐步披露情况，这种评估方式与实际使用场景存在差距。

Method: 1) 从五个Reddit社区收集求助叙述并分解为有序片段；2) 逐轮将片段呈现给语言模型；3) 使用社会支持行为编码对响应进行多标签分类；4) 通过线性探针分析隐藏表征来估计模型内部对用户困扰的感知；5) 在两个中等规模模型上进行超过6,200轮测试。

Result: 1) 支持构成随估计困扰程度系统性变化：教学支持随困扰上升而减少；2) 情感和自尊导向策略（如验证）随困扰增加而增加，但这是模型特定的且基于噪声较大的标注；3) 社区背景独立影响行为，反映主题和话语规范而非人口统计类别；4) 这些轨迹级动态在单轮评估中不可见。

Conclusion: 多轮轨迹动态揭示了单轮评估无法捕捉的模式，强调了为社交敏感应用开发多轮审计框架的必要性，以更准确地评估支持性语言模型在实际交互中的表现。

Abstract: When users seek social support from chatbots, they disclose their situation gradually, yet most evaluations of supportive LLMs rely on single-turn, fully specified prompts. We introduce a multi-turn simulation framework that closes this gap. Support-seeking narratives from five Reddit communities are decomposed into ordered fragments and revealed turn by turn to a language model. Each response is coded with the Social Support Behavior Code (SSBC), an established multi-label taxonomy that captures the composition of support, rather than a single quality score. To ask whether support choices track the model's own construal of user distress, we use linear probes on hidden representations to estimate this internal signal without altering the generation context. Across two mid-scale models (Llama-3.1-8B, OLMo-3-7B) and more than 6,200 turns, support composition shifts systematically with estimated distress: teaching declines as estimated distress rises, a finding that replicates across architectures, while increases in affective and esteem-oriented strategies (such as validation) are suggestive but model-specific and rest on noisier annotations. Community context independently shapes behavior, tracking topic and discourse norms rather than demographic categories. These trajectory-level dynamics, invisible to single-turn evaluation, motivate multi-turn auditing frameworks for socially sensitive applications.

</details>


### [50] [Comparing Human and Large Language Model Interpretation of Implicit Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17085)
*Antonio De Santis,Tommaso Bonetti,Andrea Tocchetti,Marco Brambilla*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究提出了隐式信息抽取任务，通过构建基于LLM的IIE流程来提取关系三元组、验证隐式推理和分析时间关系，发现LLM在隐式信息抽取方面覆盖有限且比人类更保守。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐式意义理解是人类交流的重要组成部分，但这一框架可能不适用于与大语言模型的交互。为了研究LLM在隐式信息抽取方面的能力，研究者提出了IIE任务。

Method: 提出了基于LLM的IIE流程，包括从上下文句子中提取关系三元组、验证隐式推理和分析时间关系。在两个数据集上评估了两个LLM，并与众包的人类判断进行比较。

Result: 人类同意大多数模型提取的三元组，但总是提出更多补充，表明当前基于LLM的IIE覆盖有限。在社交丰富的语境中，模型比人类更保守于隐式推理；而在简短的事实导向语境中，人类变得更保守。

Conclusion: LLM在隐式信息抽取方面仍有局限性，与人类在隐式推理方面存在差异。模型在社交语境中更保守，而人类在事实导向语境中更保守，这为改进LLM的隐式理解能力提供了方向。

Abstract: The interpretation of implicit meanings is an integral aspect of human communication. However, this framework may not transfer to interactions with Large Language Models (LLMs). To investigate this, we introduce the task of Implicit Information Extraction (IIE) and propose an LLM-based IIE pipeline that builds a structured knowledge graph from a context sentence by extracting relational triplets, validating implicit inferences, and analyzing temporal relations. We evaluate two LLMs against crowdsourced human judgments on two datasets. We find that humans agree with most model triplets yet consistently propose many additions, indicating limited coverage in current LLM-based IIE. Moreover, in our experiments, models appear to be more conservative about implicit inferences than humans in socially rich contexts, whereas humans become more conservative in shorter, fact-oriented contexts. Our code is available at https://github.com/Antonio-Dee/IIE_from_LLM.

</details>


### [51] [GenericAgent: A Token-Efficient Self-Evolving LLM Agent via Contextual Information Density Maximization (V1.0)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17091)
*Jiaqing Liang,Jinyi Han,Weijia Li,Xinyi Wang,Zhoujia Zhang,Zishang Jiang,Ying Liao,Tingyun Li,Ying Huang,Hao Shen,Hanyu Wu,Fang Guo,Keyi Wang,Zhonghua Hong,Zhiyu Lu,Lipeng Ma,Sihang Jiang,Yanghua Xiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GenericAgent (GA) 是一个基于上下文信息密度最大化原则构建的通用自进化LLM智能体系统，通过最小化工具集、分层按需记忆、自进化机制和上下文压缩等组件，在有限上下文预算内提升决策相关信息密度，显著优于现有智能体系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视野LLM智能体受限于上下文长度限制，随着交互变长，工具描述、检索记忆和环境反馈会挤占决策所需信息，同时任务获得的宝贵经验往往在多个任务间丢失。作者认为长视野性能不应由上下文长度决定，而应由有限上下文预算内维持的决策相关信息密度决定。

Method: GA围绕上下文信息密度最大化原则构建，包含四个紧密连接的组件：1) 最小原子工具集保持接口简洁；2) 分层按需记忆系统默认只显示高层概览；3) 自进化机制将已验证的过往轨迹转化为可重用的标准操作程序(SOP)和可执行代码；4) 上下文截断和压缩层在长执行过程中维持信息密度。

Result: 在任务完成、工具使用效率、记忆有效性、自进化和网页浏览等方面，GA始终优于领先的智能体系统，同时使用显著更少的token和交互次数，并且能够随时间持续进化。

Conclusion: 长视野LLM智能体的性能瓶颈在于上下文信息密度而非上下文长度，通过上下文信息密度最大化原则构建的GenericAgent系统能够有效提升智能体性能，减少资源消耗，并具备持续进化的能力。

Abstract: Long-horizon large language model (LLM) agents are fundamentally limited by context. As interactions become longer, tool descriptions, retrieved memories, and raw environmental feedback accumulate and push out the information needed for decision-making. At the same time, useful experience gained from tasks is often lost across episodes. We argue that long-horizon performance is determined not by context length, but by how much decision-relevant information is maintained within a finite context budget. We present GenericAgent (GA), a general-purpose, self-evolving LLM agent system built around a single principle: context information density maximization. GA implements this through four closely connected components: a minimal atomic tool set that keeps the interface simple, a hierarchical on-demand memory that only shows a small high-level view by default, a self-evolution mechanism that turns verified past trajectories into reusable SOPs and executable code, and a context truncation and compression layer that maintains information density during long executions. Across task completion, tool use efficiency, memory effectiveness, self-evolution, and web browsing, GA consistently outperforms leading agent systems while using significantly fewer tokens and interactions, and it continues to evolve over time. Project: https://github.com/lsdefine/GenericAgent

</details>


### [52] [Beyond Word Boundaries: A Hebrew Coreference Benchmark and an Evaluation Protocol for Morphologically Complex Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17108)
*Refael Shaked Greenfeld,Reut Tsarfaty*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文针对形态丰富语言（MRLs）中的指代消解问题，构建了首个现代希伯来语指代消解数据集KibutzR，提出了解决词/语素边界不一致的评估协议，发现当代LLMs在希伯来语上表现显著差于英语，且小编码器模型表现优于解码器模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指代消解是NLP基础任务，但现有方法主要针对英语设计，在形态丰富语言（MRLs）中面临挑战：提及边界不一定与词边界对齐，单个词可能包含多个指代。英语中词与提及基本对齐的假设在MRLs中不成立，特别是在LLMs的原始文本处理和端到端任务中。

Method: 1. 构建首个现代希伯来语指代消解数据集KibutzR，在词、子词和多词层面标注提及；2. 提出直接解决词/语素边界不一致的评估协议；3. 实验评估当代LLMs在希伯来语和英语上的表现差异，分析原始未分词文本的影响。

Result: 1. 当代LLMs在希伯来语上的表现显著差于英语；2. 在原始未分词文本上性能下降；3. 希伯来语中呈现与英语相反的趋势：较小的编码器模型表现远优于当代解码器模型；4. 为希伯来语指代消解提供了新基准和分词感知的评估协议。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了形态丰富语言中指代消解的特殊挑战，提供了希伯来语指代消解基准数据集和评估协议，展示了LLMs在MRLs上的局限性，为未来研究提供了改进空间和方向。

Abstract: Coreference Resolution (CR) is a fundamental NLP task critical for long-form tasks as information extraction, summarization, and many business applications. However, CR methods originally designed for English struggle with Morphologically Rich Languages (MRLs), where mention boundaries do not necessarily align with word boundaries, and a single token may consist of multiple anaphors. CR modeling and evaluation protocols standardly assume that, as in English, words and mentions mostly align. However, this assumption breaks down in MRLs, particularly in the context of LLMs' raw-text processing and end-to-end tasks. To assess and address this challenge, we introduce {\em KibutzR}, the first comprehensive CR dataset for Modern Hebrew, an MRL rich with complex words and pronominal clitics. We deliver an annotated dataset that identifies mentions at word, sub-word and multi-word levels, and propose an evaluation protocol that directly addresses word/morpheme boundary discrepancies. Our experiments show that contemporary LLMs perform significantly worse on Hebrew than on English, and that performance degrades on raw unsegmented text. Crucially, we show an inverse performance-trend in Hebrew relative to English, where smaller encoders perform far better than contemporary decoder models, leaving ample space for investigation and improvement. We deliver a new benchmark for Hebrew coreference resolution and a segmentation-aware evaluation protocol to inform future work on other MRLs.

</details>


### [53] [The Provenance Gap in Clinical AI: Evidence-Traceable Temporal Knowledge Graphs for Rare Disease Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17114)
*Md Shamim Ahmed,Maja Dusanic,Moritz Nikolai Kirschner,Elisabeth Nyoungui,Jana Zschüntzsch,Lukas Galke Poech,Richard Röttger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了前沿大语言模型在临床应用中存在"出处差距"问题——模型能生成准确的临床输出但引用常被伪造。研究者开发了HEG-TKG系统，通过分层证据锚定的时间知识图谱实现100%可验证的引用，显著优于传统RAG方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿大语言模型虽然能生成临床准确的输出，但其引用经常被伪造，这被称为"出处差距"。在临床决策中，可验证的证据来源至关重要，而现有模型无法提供可靠的引用支持，这限制了其在医疗领域的实际应用。

Method: 研究测试了5个前沿LLM在36个临床验证场景中的表现，发现无模型能生成相关PubMed标识符。为解决此问题，研究者开发了HEG-TKG系统：构建基于4,512篇PubMed记录的分层证据锚定时间知识图谱，包含质量层级分层和1,280个疾病轨迹里程碑。系统采用三臂对比实验设计，使用相同合成模型比较不同方法。

Result: 前沿LLM在无提示时无法生成相关PubMed标识符；最佳模型在明确要求引用时仅达到15.3%相关PMID。HEG-TKG系统实现100%证据可验证性，包含203个内联引用，而Guideline-RAG方法产生零可验证引用。临床医生评估确认可验证性优势显著（Cohen's d = 1.81, p < 0.001），安全性或完整性无下降。反事实实验显示80%对注入临床错误的抵抗力，100%可通过引用追踪检测。

Conclusion: HEG-TKG系统通过分层证据锚定的时间知识图谱有效解决了LLM在临床应用中的"出处差距"问题，实现了完全可验证的引用，同时保持临床准确性和安全性。该系统可在本地部署，确保患者数据不离开机构基础设施，为临床决策支持提供了可靠的技术方案。

Abstract: Frontier large language models generate clinically accurate outputs, but their citations are often fabricated. We term this the Provenance Gap. We tested five frontier LLMs across 36 clinician-validated scenarios for three rare neuromuscular disease pairs. No model produced a clinically relevant PubMed identifier without prompting. When explicitly asked to cite, the best model achieved 15.3% relevant PMIDs; the majority resolved to real publications in unrelated fields. We present HEG-TKG (Hierarchical Evidence-Grounded Temporal Knowledge Graphs), a system that grounds clinical claims in temporal knowledge graphs built from 4,512 PubMed records and curated sources with quality-tier stratification and 1,280 disease-trajectory milestones. In a controlled three-arm comparison using the same synthesis model, HEG-TKG matches baseline clinical feature coverage while achieving 100% evidence verifiability with 203 inline citations. Guideline-RAG, given overlapping source documents as raw text, produces zero verifiable citations. LLM judges cannot distinguish fabricated from verified citations without PubMed audit data. Independent clinician evaluation confirms the verifiability advantage (Cohen's d = 1.81, p < 0.001) with no degradation on safety or completeness. A counterfactual experiment shows 80% resistance to injected clinical errors with 100% detectability via citation trace. The system deploys on-premise via open-source models so patient data never leaves institutional infrastructure.

</details>


### [54] [Please refuse to answer me! Mitigating Over-Refusal in Large Language Models via Adaptive Contrastive Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17132)
*Yupeng Qi,Ziyu Lyu,Lixin Cui,Lu Bai,Feng Xia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: AdaCD是一种无需训练、模型无关的方法，通过对比解码策略解决LLM过度拒绝问题，在保持安全性的同时减少对无害查询的拒绝率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全对齐的大语言模型存在过度拒绝问题，对无害查询也生成拒绝响应。现有方法难以在保持对恶意查询高拒绝率的同时，降低对无害查询的拒绝率

Method: 提出自适应对比解码(AdaCD)：1) 比较有无极端安全系统提示时的输出分布，精炼拒绝令牌分布；2) 引入自适应对比解码策略，动态调整拒绝令牌分布，自适应提升拒绝或非拒绝令牌的选择概率

Result: 在五个基准数据集上的实验结果显示，AdaCD平均将过度拒绝查询的拒绝率降低10.35%，同时仍将恶意查询的拒绝率提高0.13%

Conclusion: AdaCD能有效缓解LLM的过度拒绝问题，同时保持模型的安全性，是一种无需训练、模型无关的实用解决方案

Abstract: Safety-aligned large language models (LLMs) often generate refusal responses to harmless queries due to the over-refusal problem. However, existing methods for mitigating over-refusal cannot maintain a low refusal ratio for harmless queries while keeping a high refusal ratio for malicious ones. In this paper, we analyze how system prompts with varying safety levels affect LLM refusal behaviors when facing over-refusal queries. A key observation is that, when LLMs suffer from the over-refusal issue, non-refusal tokens remain present in the next-token candidate list, but the model systematically fails to select them, despite the generation of refusal tokens. Based on this observation, we propose a training-free and model-agnostic approach, Adaptive Contrastive Decoding (AdaCD), to mitigate over-refusal while maintaining LLM safety. First, AdaCD compares the output distributions of the LLM with or without an extreme safety system prompt to refine the refusal token distribution. Second, we introduce an adaptive contrastive decoding strategy that dynamically incorporates or removes the refusal token distribution, adaptively boosting the probability of selecting refusal or non-refusal tokens. Experimental results on five benchmark datasets show that, on average, AdaCD reduces the refusal ratio for over-refusal queries by 10.35%, yet still increases the refusal ratio for malicious queries by 0.13%. Code is available at https://github.com/OutdoorManofML/AdaCD.

</details>


### [55] [RoIt-XMASA: Multi-Domain Multilingual Sentiment Analysis Dataset for Romanian and Italian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17134)
*Andrei-Marius Avram,Aureliu Valentin Antonie,Cosmin-Mircea Croitoru,Vlad Andrei Muntean,Dumitru-Clementin Cercel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RoIt-XMASA扩展了跨语言多领域亚马逊情感分析数据集到意大利语和罗马尼亚语，提出多目标对抗训练框架，通过元学习系数动态平衡情感判别与领域/语言不变性，在跨语言跨领域情感分析任务上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨语言情感分析数据集在意大利语和罗马尼亚语等语言上覆盖不足，同时需要解决跨语言和跨领域的双重挑战，即如何在保持情感判别能力的同时实现领域和语言的不变性。

Method: 1) 构建RoIt-XMASA数据集：包含36,000个标注评论（书籍、电影、音乐三个领域）和202,141个未标注样本；2) 提出多目标对抗训练框架：使用损失反转和元学习系数动态平衡情感判别损失与领域/语言不变性损失。

Result: XLM-R模型使用该方法获得66.23%的F1分数，比基线提升4.64%。少样本评估显示Llama-3.1-8B达到58.43% F1分数，揭示了基于提示的方法的效率与任务特定微调的高性能之间的权衡。

Conclusion: RoIt-XMASA数据集填补了意大利语和罗马尼亚语跨语言情感分析资源的空白，提出的多目标对抗训练框架有效解决了跨语言和跨领域的挑战，为资源有限语言的情感分析提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: We present RoIt-XMASA, a multilingual dataset that extends the Cross-lingual Multi-domain Amazon Sentiment Analysis to Italian and Romanian, comprising 36,000 labeled reviews across three domains (books, movies, and music) and 202,141 unlabeled samples. To address cross-lingual and cross-domain challenges, we propose a multi-target adversarial training framework that employs loss reversal with meta-learned coefficients to dynamically balance sentiment discrimination with domain and language invariance. XLM-R achieves an F1-score of 66.23% with our approach, outperforming the baseline by 4.64%. Few-shot evaluation shows that Llama-3.1-8B achieves 58.43% F1-score, revealing a meaningful trade-off between the efficiency of prompting-based approaches and the higher performance of task-specific fine-tuning.

</details>


### [56] [The Consensus Trap: Rescuing Multi-Agent LLMs from Adversarial Majorities via Token-Level Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17139)
*Jiayuan Liu,Shiyi Du,Weihua Du,Mingyu Guo,Vincent Conitzer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 多智能体LLM系统面临上下文污染攻击时，基于投票的响应级聚合存在结构性漏洞，本文提出令牌级轮询协作机制，通过理论证明和实验验证其在多数智能体被污染时仍能保持鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前多智能体LLM系统依赖响应级聚合（如多数投票）来提升推理能力，但在开放环境中，智能体容易受到隐蔽的上下文污染攻击（如针对性提示注入）。研究发现当被污染的智能体形成局部多数时，响应级聚合会崩溃，因为投票只关注最终结论而忽略有缺陷的中间逻辑。

Method: 提出令牌级轮询协作机制，智能体在共享的自回归上下文中顺序交错生成令牌。将这一过程形式化为离散时间动态系统，证明令牌级交错将聚合从脆弱的最终投票计数（线性求和）转变为动态、交织的逻辑链（非线性算子乘积）。

Result: 理论分析表明，即使被污染的智能体形成多数，诚实模型的恢复性拉力也能克服对抗性污染。在多个推理基准测试中的实证评估显示，当被污染智能体达到多数时，多数投票方法崩溃，而轮询协作机制在超过这一临界阈值后仍能保持鲁棒准确性。

Conclusion: 令牌级轮询协作机制通过将聚合从响应级转移到令牌级，从根本上解决了多智能体LLM系统的结构性漏洞，即使在多数智能体被污染的情况下也能维持系统鲁棒性，为构建更安全的协作AI系统提供了新方向。

Abstract: Multi-agent large language model (LLM) architectures increasingly rely on response-level aggregation, such as Majority Voting (MAJ), to raise reasoning ceilings. However, in open environments, agents are highly susceptible to stealthy contextual corruption, such as targeted prompt injections. We reveal a critical structural vulnerability in current multi-agent systems: response-level aggregation collapses when corrupted agents form a local majority. Because voting aggregates fully-formed conclusions, it is blind to flawed intermediate logic. To overcome this systematic limitation, we propose the Token-Level Round-Robin (RR) Collaboration, where agents sequentially interleave generation within a shared auto-regressive context. We formalize this process as a discrete-time dynamical system, proving that token-level interleaving transitions aggregation from a brittle counting of final votes (a linear sum) to a dynamic, interwoven chain of logic (a non-linear operator product). Through this theoretical lens, we prove that the honest model's restorative pull can overpower adversarial corruptions, even when corrupted agents form a majority. We conduct an exhaustive empirical evaluation across diverse reasoning benchmarks and demonstrate that while MAJ collapses when corrupted agents reach a majority, RR maintains robust accuracy well beyond this critical threshold.

</details>


### [57] [From Legal Text to Executable Decision Models: Evaluating Structured Representations for Legal Decision Model Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17153)
*David Graus*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究使用LLM将荷兰环境规划法文本转换为可执行决策模型，发现添加输入输出约束能显著提升生成质量，但结构相似性与功能等价性并不完全一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将法律文本转换为可执行决策逻辑是法律信息学的长期挑战。虽然LLM带来了新的可能性，但仍需要大量手动编码和评估。研究旨在探索结构化中间表示是否能改进LLM从法律文本生成可执行决策模型的能力。

Method: 使用荷兰环境规划法的真实数据集，比较四种输入条件：原始法律文本、添加语义角色标签的文本、添加输入输出约束的文本、以及两者都添加的文本。通过结构评估（使用图核和描述性统计）和结果评估（通过预配置测试场景执行模型）两个维度进行评价。

Result: 输入输出约束提供了主要改进（比基线提高37-54%相似性），语义角色标签改进有限。生成的模型在51-53%的测试场景中与黄金标准匹配，尽管生成的模型通常更小更简单。LLM消除了占节点45-55%的冗余传递逻辑。结构相似性和结果等价性是互补的，并不相互保证。

Conclusion: 结构化中间表示特别是输入输出约束能显著提升LLM从法律文本生成可执行决策模型的质量。研究公开了95个生产决策模型的数据集和实验代码，促进了可重复性研究。

Abstract: Transforming legal text into executable decision logic is a longstanding challenge in legal informatics. With the rise of LLMs, this task has gained renewed interest, but remains challenging due to requiring extensive manual coding and evaluation. We use a unique real-world dataset that pairs production-grade decision models with legal text from the Dutch Environment and Planning Act. These models power the Omgevingsloket government platform, where citizens check permit requirements for environmental activities. We study whether intermediate structured representations can improve LLM-based generation of executable decision models from legal text. We compare four input conditions: raw legal text, text enriched with semantic role labels, text enriched with input and output constraints, and text enriched with both. We evaluate along two dimensions: structural evaluation, through similarity to gold decision models with graph kernels and graphs' descriptive statistics, and outcome evaluation, through functional equivalence by executing models on pre-configured test scenarios. Our findings show that I/O constraints provide the dominant improvement (+37-54% similarity over baseline), while semantic role labels show modest improvements. Outcome evaluation shows that generated models match the gold standard on 51-53% of test scenarios, even though generated models are typically smaller and simpler. We find LLMs eliminate redundant pass-through logic that comprises up to 45-55% of nodes. Importantly, structural similarity and outcome equivalence are complementary: structural similarity does not guarantee outcome equivalence, and vice versa. To facilitate reproducibility, we publicly release our dataset of 95 production decision models with associated legal text and all experimental code.

</details>


### [58] [Modeling Multi-Dimensional Cognitive States in Large Language Models under Cognitive Crowding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17174)
*Lin Zhong,Siyu Zhu,Zizhen Yuan,Jinhao Cui,Xinyang Zhao,Lingzhi Wang,Hao Chen,Qing Liao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文构建了首个跨情感、思维风格、立场和意图四个认知维度的统一评测基准CognitiveBench，发现LLMs在多维联合建模时性能显著下降，并提出在双曲空间中建模认知状态的HyCoLLM方法，使8B参数模型超越GPT-4o等基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型主要处理情感分析或立场检测等孤立任务，未能捕捉心理学定义的多个认知维度（情感、思维风格、立场、意图）之间的相互作用。需要构建统一评测基准并解决LLMs在多维认知建模中的性能瓶颈。

Method: 1) 构建CognitiveBench基准，统一标注四个认知维度；2) 使用Gromov δ-双曲性分析揭示基准的层次结构；3) 提出HyCoLLM方法，在双曲空间中建模认知状态，通过双曲引导对齐调优对齐LLM表示。

Result: 实验显示：1) LLMs在单维度任务表现良好，但在多维联合建模时性能急剧下降；2) CognitiveBench表现出强层次结构；3) HyCoLLM显著提升多维认知理解能力，8B参数模型超越包括GPT-4o在内的强基线。

Conclusion: 认知状态具有层次结构，在欧几里得空间中建模会导致"认知拥挤"问题。通过在双曲空间中建模认知状态并采用双曲引导对齐调优，可以有效解决表示空间不匹配问题，显著提升LLMs的多维认知理解能力。

Abstract: Modeling human cognitive states is essential for advanced artificial intelligence. Existing Large Language Models (LLMs) mainly address isolated tasks such as emotion analysis or stance detection, and fail to capture interactions among cognitive dimensions defined in psychology, including emotion, thinking style, stance, and intention. To bridge this gap, we construct CognitiveBench, the first benchmark with unified annotations across the above four dimensions. Experiments on CognitiveBench show that although LLMs perform well on single dimension tasks, their performance drops sharply in joint multi-dimensional modeling. Using Gromov $δ$-hyperbolicity analysis, we find that CognitiveBench exhibits a strong hierarchical structure. We attribute the performance bottleneck to ``Cognitive Crowding'', where hierarchical cognitive states require exponential representational space, while the Euclidean space of LLMs grows only polynomially, causing representation overlap and degraded performance. To address this mismatch, we propose HyCoLLM, which models cognitive states in hyperbolic space and aligns LLM representations via Hyperbolic Guided Alignment Tuning. Results show that HyCoLLM substantially improves multi-dimensional cognitive understanding, allowing 8B parameter model to outperform strong baselines, including GPT-4o.

</details>


### [59] [Cognitive Policy-Driven LLM for Diagnosis and Intervention of Cognitive Distortions in Emotional Support Conversation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17178)
*Lin Zhong,Renjin Zhu,Shujuan Ma,Jinhao Cui,Lingzhi Wang,Hao Chen,Qing Liao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文针对情感支持对话中忽视求助者认知扭曲的问题，构建了首个包含认知扭曲标签的数据集CogBiasESC，并提出了CoPoLLM框架来增强LLM诊断和干预认知扭曲的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情感支持对话方法忽视了求助者表达中的认知扭曲问题，导致模型只能提供基础情感安慰，无法在更深层次上帮助求助者解决心理困扰。

Method: 1. 构建CogBiasESC数据集，扩展现有ESC数据集，添加认知扭曲的类型、强度和安全风险等级标签；2. 提出Cognitive Policy-driven Large Language Model框架(CoPoLLM)，增强LLM诊断和干预认知扭曲的能力。

Result: CoPoLLM在认知扭曲诊断准确性、干预策略有效性和安全风险控制方面显著优于15个最先进的基线模型。

Conclusion: 该研究通过构建专门数据集和提出CoPoLLM框架，有效解决了情感支持对话中认知扭曲的诊断和干预问题，提升了LLM在心理支持领域的深度应用能力。

Abstract: Emotional Support Conversation (ESC) plays a critical role in mental health assistance by providing accessible psychological support in real-world applications. Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown strong empathetic abilities in ESC tasks. Yet, existing methods overlook the issue of cognitive distortions in help-seekers' expressions. As a result, current models can only provide basic emotional comfort, rather than helping help-seekers address their psychological distress at a deeper cognitive level. To address this challenge, we construct the CogBiasESC dataset, the first dataset that expands existing ESC datasets by adding labels for cognitive distortions, includes their type, intensity, and safe risk level. Furthermore, we propose the Cognitive Policy-driven Large Language Model framework (CoPoLLM) to enhance LLMs' ability to diagnose and intervene cognitive distortions in help-seekers. We also analyze the safety advantages of CoPoLLM from a theoretical perspective. Experimental results show that CoPoLLM significantly outperforms 15 state-of-the-art baselines in terms of distortion diagnosis accuracy, intervention strategy effectiveness, and safety risk control.

</details>


### [60] [Beyond Overlap Metrics: Rewarding Reasoning and Preferences for Faithful Multi-Role Dialogue Summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17188)
*Xiaoyong Mei,Tingting Zuo,Da Chen,Guangyu Hu,Xiangyu Wen,Chao Duan,Mingyan Zhang,Fudan Zheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出结合认知推理与奖励优化的多角色对话摘要框架，通过推理监督和双原则奖励提升事实一致性和人类偏好对齐


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法过度依赖ROUGE等表面指标，忽视了事实一致性和人类偏好的真正改进，需要更可靠的多角色对话摘要方法

Method: 1) 从大教师模型提取结构化推理轨迹作为辅助监督，通过分阶段监督微调初始化推理感知摘要器；2) 应用GRPO结合双原则奖励，融合指标信号与人类对齐标准（关键信息覆盖、隐式推理、事实忠实性、简洁性）

Result: 在多语言多角色对话基准上，方法在ROUGE和BERTScore上匹配强基线；CSDS结果显示语义一致性稳定，SAMSum分析显示事实忠实性和模型偏好对齐明显提升

Conclusion: 推理感知和偏好感知训练对可靠对话摘要具有重要价值，提出的框架能有效提升摘要质量

Abstract: Multi-role dialogue summarization requires modeling complex interactions among multiple speakers while preserving role-specific information and factual consistency. However, most existing methods optimize for automatic metrics such as ROUGE and BERTScore, which favor surface-level imitation of references rather than genuine gains in faithfulness or alignment with human preferences. We propose a novel framework that couples explicit cognitive-style reasoning with reward-based optimization for multi-role dialogue summarization. Our method first distills structured reasoning traces (e.g., step-by-step inferences and intermediate reflections) from a large teacher model and uses them as auxiliary supervision to initialize a reasoning-aware summarizer via staged supervised fine-tuning. It then applies GRPO with a dual-principle reward that blends metric-based signals with human-aligned criteria targeting key information coverage, implicit inference, factual faithfulness, and conciseness. Experiments on multilingual multi-role dialogue benchmarks show that our method matches strong baselines on ROUGE and BERTScore. Specifically, results on CSDS confirm the framework's stability in semantic consistency, while in-depth analysis on SAMSum demonstrates clear gains in factual faithfulness and model-based preference alignment. These findings underscore the value of reasoning-aware and preference-aware training for reliable dialogue summarization. Checkpoints and datasets are available at https://huggingface.co/collections/NebulaPixel/summorchestra-multirole-summary.

</details>


### [61] [Learning to Control Summaries with Score Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17197)
*Hongye Liu,Liang Ding,Ricardo Henao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种基于模型评估分数的损失函数，能在提升摘要整体质量的同时，实现对特定质量维度（如完整性、简洁性）的精确控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有摘要研究主要关注同时优化多个质量维度，但忽视了在存在固有权衡关系时对单个维度的控制能力。例如，提高简洁性可能损害完整性，反之亦然。

Method: 提出一种损失函数，将模型输出与细粒度的模型评估分数（如FineSurE）对齐，既能提升摘要质量，又能实现维度特定的控制。

Result: 在三个预训练模型（LLaMA、Qwen、Mistral）上的实验表明，该方法在保持与最先进摘要模型相当性能的同时，独特地提供了对单个质量维度的强可控性。

Conclusion: 该方法填补了摘要研究中维度控制能力的空白，实现了在提升整体质量的同时，能够选择性地优先考虑某一标准而非其他标准。

Abstract: Recent advances in summarization research focus on improving summary quality across multiple criteria, such as completeness, conciseness, and faithfulness, by jointly optimizing these dimensions. However, these efforts largely overlook the challenge of controlling summary generation with respect to individual criteria, especially in the presence of their inherent trade-offs. For example, enhancing conciseness can compromise completeness, and vice versa. In this work, we address this gap by proposing a loss function that aligns model outputs with fine-grained, model-based evaluation scores (e.g., from FineSurE), enabling both improvement in summary quality and dimension-specific control. Our approach improves the overall quality of summaries while maintaining the ability to selectively prioritize one criterion over others. Experiments on three pretrained models (LLaMA, Qwen, and Mistral) demonstrate that our method achieves performance comparable to state-of-the-art summarizers, while uniquely offering strong controllability over individual quality dimensions.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [62] [UniMamba: A Unified Spatial-Temporal Modeling Framework with State-Space and Attention Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16325)
*Xingsheng Chen,Xianpei Mu,Deyu Yi,Yilin Yuan,Xingwei He,Bo Gao,Regina Zhang,Pietro Lio,Siu-Ming Yiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: UniMamba是一个统一时空预测框架，结合状态空间模型的高效长上下文建模和注意力机制的依赖学习，用于多元时间序列预测，在准确性和计算效率上均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多元时间序列预测在能源、金融、环境监测等领域至关重要，但面临复杂时间依赖和跨变量交互的挑战。现有Transformer方法计算成本高（二次复杂度），而Mamba等状态空间模型虽能高效建模长上下文，但缺乏显式的时间模式识别能力。

Method: UniMamba采用三部分架构：1) Mamba Variate-Channel Encoding Layer（增强FFT-Laplace变换和TCN）捕获全局时间依赖；2) Spatial Temporal Attention Layer联合建模变量间相关性和时间演化；3) Feedforward Temporal Dynamics Layer融合连续和离散上下文进行准确预测。

Result: 在8个公共基准数据集上的综合实验表明，UniMamba在预测准确性和计算效率方面均持续优于最先进的预测模型，为长序列多元时间序列预测提供了可扩展且稳健的解决方案。

Conclusion: UniMamba成功整合了高效状态空间动力学和基于注意力的依赖学习，解决了现有方法的局限性，为多元时间序列预测提供了既准确又高效的统一框架。

Abstract: Multivariate time series forecasting is fundamental to numerous domains such as energy, finance, and environmental monitoring, where complex temporal dependencies and cross-variable interactions pose enduring challenges. Existing Transformer-based methods capture temporal correlations through attention mechanisms but suffer from quadratic computational cost, while state-space models like Mamba achieve efficient long-context modeling yet lack explicit temporal pattern recognition. Therefore we introduce UniMamba, a unified spatial-temporal forecasting framework that integrates efficient state-space dynamics with attention-based dependency learning. UniMamba employs a Mamba Variate-Channel Encoding Layer enhanced with FFT-Laplace Transform and TCN to capture global temporal dependencies, and a Spatial Temporal Attention Layer to jointly model inter-variate correlations and temporal evolution. A Feedforward Temporal Dynamics Layer further fuses continuous and discrete contexts for accurate forecasting. Comprehensive experiments on eight public benchmark datasets demonstrate that UniMamba consistently outperforms state-of-the-art forecasting models in both forecasting accuracy and computational efficiency, establishing a scalable and robust solution for long-sequence multivariate time-series prediction.

</details>


### [63] [Annotation Entropy Predicts Per-Example Learning Dynamics in LoRA Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16332)
*Brady Steele*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LoRA微调在争议性示例上表现出"遗忘"现象：标注者分歧高的样本在训练过程中损失持续增加，这与全参数微调不同，在测试的6个模型中都存在


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究LoRA微调方法在存在标注分歧的数据上的行为差异，探索参数高效微调方法对争议性示例的处理方式

Method: 使用ChaosNLI的100个标注计算标注熵，与SNLI和MNLI上每个示例的损失曲线下面积(AULC)相关分析，测试了4个编码器模型和2个仅解码器模型

Result: 在25个测试条件下都观察到正相关(Spearman ρ=0.06-0.43)，仅解码器模型在相同LoRA秩下表现出更强的相关性，效应在部分相关控制和不同种子、数据集上都能复现

Conclusion: LoRA微调在争议性示例上表现出独特的"遗忘"模式，这与全参数微调不同，表明参数高效微调方法在处理标注分歧数据时存在系统性差异

Abstract: We find that LoRA fine-tuning exhibits un-learning on contested examples: items with high annotator disagreement show increasing loss during training, a qualitatively distinct pattern largely absent under full fine-tuning and consistent across all six models tested (four encoder, two decoder-only). This discovery emerges from correlating annotation entropy, computed from ChaosNLI's 100 labels per example, with per-example area under the loss curve (AULC) on SNLI and MNLI. The correlation is positive in all 25 conditions tested (Spearman $ρ= 0.06$-$0.43$), with decoder-only models showing stronger correlations than encoders at matched LoRA rank. The effect survives partial-correlation controls and replicates across seeds and datasets. A preliminary noise-injection experiment is consistent with these findings.

</details>


### [64] [SaFeR-Steer: Evolving Multi-Turn MLLMs via Synthetic Bootstrapping and Feedback Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16358)
*Haolong Hu,Hanyu Li,Tiancheng He,Huahui Yi,An Zhang,Qiankun Li,Kun Wang,Yang Liu,Zhigang Zeng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SaFeR-Steer是一个渐进式多轮对齐框架，通过分阶段合成引导和导师在环GRPO训练，解决多轮对话中安全意图升级和长上下文安全衰减问题，显著提升模型在单轮和多轮场景下的安全性和有用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前MLLMs越来越多地部署在多轮对话场景中，攻击者可以通过演化的视觉-文本历史逐步升级不安全意图，并利用长上下文安全衰减漏洞。然而现有的安全对齐主要基于单轮数据和固定模板对话，导致训练与部署之间存在不匹配。

Method: 提出SaFeR-Steer渐进式多轮对齐框架，结合分阶段合成引导和导师在环GRPO训练单个学生模型，使其能够适应自适应、在策略的攻击。同时引入TCSR方法，使用轨迹最小/平均安全度将后期轮次的失败传播到早期轮次。

Result: 基于Qwen2.5-VL-3B/7B模型，SaFeR-Steer显著提升了单轮和多轮基准测试的安全性和有用性：3B模型单轮从48.30/45.86提升到81.84/70.77，多轮从12.55/27.13提升到55.58/70.27；7B模型单轮从56.21/60.32提升到87.89/77.40，多轮从24.66/46.48提升到64.89/72.35，将失败推迟到后期轮次，展现出超越单纯扩展的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SaFeR-Steer框架有效解决了多轮对话中的安全对齐问题，通过渐进式训练和轨迹安全传播机制，显著提升了MLLMs在多轮场景下的安全性和有用性，并发布了STEER多轮多模态安全数据集，为相关研究提供了重要资源。

Abstract: MLLMs are increasingly deployed in multi-turn settings, where attackers can escalate unsafe intent through the evolving visual-text history and exploit long-context safety decay. Yet safety alignment is still dominated by single-turn data and fixed-template dialogues, leaving a mismatch between training and deployment.To bridge this gap, we propose SaFeR-Steer, a progressive multi-turn alignment framework that combines staged synthetic bootstrapping with tutor-in-the-loop GRPO to train a single student under adaptive, on-policy attacks. We also introduce TCSR, which uses trajectory minimum/average safety to propagate late-turn failures to earlier turns.I. Dataset. We release STEER, a multi-turn multimodal safety dataset with STEER-SFT (12,934), STEER-RL (2,000), and STEER-Bench (3,227) dialogues spanning 2~10 turns.II. Experiment. Starting from Qwen2.5-VL-3B/7B, SaFeR-Steer substantially improves Safety/Helpfulness on both single-turn (48.30/45.86 -> 81.84/70.77 for 3B; 56.21/60.32 -> 87.89/77.40 for 7B) and multi-turn benchmarks (12.55/27.13 -> 55.58/70.27 for 3B; 24.66/46.48 -> 64.89/72.35 for 7B), shifting failures to later turns and yielding robustness beyond scaling alone.Codes are available at https://github.com/Ed-Bg/SaFeR-Steer

</details>


### [65] [SetFlow: Generating Structured Sets of Representations for Multiple Instance Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16362)
*Nikola Jovišić,Milica Škipina,Vanja Švenda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SetFlow是一种用于多示例学习（MIL）的生成架构，直接在表示空间中建模整个MIL包（集合），通过流匹配和Set Transformer设计处理置换不变输入，用于数据稀缺场景下的增强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在乳腺X光摄影等实际应用中，数据稀缺和弱监督限制了机器学习模型的性能。虽然基础模型提供了强大的语义表示，但现有增强方法在实例级别操作，无法捕捉包内实例间的依赖关系，限制了MIL数据的有效增强。

Method: 提出SetFlow生成架构，结合流匹配范式和Set Transformer设计，直接在表示空间中建模整个MIL包。模型以类别标签和输入规模为条件，能够生成连贯且语义一致的表示集合，处理置换不变的输入并捕捉包内实例间的交互。

Result: 在大规模乳腺X光摄影基准测试中，使用最先进的MIL-PF分类管道评估SetFlow。生成的样本与原始数据分布高度匹配，用于增强时甚至能提高下游性能。仅使用合成数据训练也显示出有竞争力的结果。

Conclusion: SetFlow证明了表示空间生成建模在数据稀缺和隐私敏感任务中的有效性，能够生成与原始分布匹配的MIL包表示，提高下游分类性能，为弱监督学习场景提供了有效的增强解决方案。

Abstract: Data scarcity and weak supervision continue to limit the performance of machine learning models in many real-world applications, such as mammography, where Multiple Instance Learning (MIL) often offers the best formulation. While recent foundation models provide strong semantic representations out of the box, effective augmentation of such representations of MIL data remains limited, as existing methods operate at the instance level and fail to capture intra-bag dependencies. In this work, we introduce SetFlow, a generative architecture that models entire MIL bags (i.e., sets) directly in the representation space. Our approach leverages the flow matching paradigm combined with a Set Transformer-inspired design, enabling it to handle permutation-invariant inputs while capturing interactions between instances within each bag. The model is conditioned on both class labels and input scale, allowing it to generate coherent and semantically consistent sets of representations. We evaluate SetFlow on a large-scale mammography benchmark using a state-of-the-art MIL-PF classification pipeline. The generated samples are shown to closely match the original data distribution and even improve downstream performance when used for augmentation. Furthermore, training on synthetic data alone shows competitive results, demonstrating the effectiveness of representation-space generative modeling for data-scarce and privacy-sensitive tasks.

</details>


### [66] [Matched-Learning-Rate Analysis of Attention Drift and Transfer Retention in Fine-Tuned CLIP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16410)
*Ruize Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CLIP模型微调研究中，通过匹配学习率对比Full FT和LoRA，发现学习率对注意力漂移和跨域迁移能力有显著影响，LoRA在保持零样本迁移能力方面明显优于Full FT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究CLIP模型微调中，传统比较Full FT和LoRA时学习率设置不一致导致结论混淆的问题，需要控制学习率变量来准确评估两种方法对注意力漂移和跨域迁移能力的影响。

Method: 采用匹配学习率控制实验设计，在CLIP ViT-B/32模型上对EuroSAT和Oxford-IIIT Pets数据集进行80次实验，涵盖4个共享学习率和5个随机种子，评估注意力漂移指标、最佳验证准确率和适配器感知的CIFAR-100零样本准确率。

Result: 学习率强烈调节结构变化：在EuroSAT上，Full FT从低学习率的轻度熵增到高学习率的显著熵减，而LoRA在整个学习率范围内保持熵增。在匹配学习率下，LoRA保持的零样本迁移能力显著优于Full FT（EuroSAT: 45.13% vs 11.28%，Pets: 58.01% vs 8.54%）。

Conclusion: 匹配学习率评估显著改变了Full FT与LoRA的比较解释，注意力漂移更适合作为表征保持的描述性诊断指标，而非迁移行为的因果解释。LoRA在保持跨域迁移能力方面具有明显优势。

Abstract: CLIP adaptation can improve in-domain accuracy while degrading out-of-domain transfer, but comparisons between Full Fine-Tuning (Full FT) and LoRA are often confounded by different learning-rate conventions. We study how adaptation method and optimization scale jointly shape attention drift and transfer retention in CLIP using a controlled matched-learning-rate comparison of Full FT and LoRA. The completed matrix contains 80 runs on CLIP ViT-B/32 across EuroSAT and Oxford-IIIT Pets, spanning four shared learning rates ($10^{-6}$, $5{\times}10^{-6}$, $10^{-5}$, $5{\times}10^{-5}$) and five seeds, and evaluates attention-drift metrics, best validation accuracy, and adapter-aware CIFAR-100 zero-shot accuracy. Learning rate strongly modulates structural change: on EuroSAT, Full FT moves from mild entropy broadening at $10^{-6}$ to marked contraction at $5{\times}10^{-5}$, whereas LoRA remains entropy-positive across the full matched grid. At matched learning rates, LoRA preserves substantially more zero-shot transfer than Full FT, averaging $45.13\%$ versus $11.28\%$ CIFAR-100 accuracy on EuroSAT and $58.01\%$ versus $8.54\%$ on Pets. Oxford-IIIT Pets also reveals a regime effect: low-learning-rate LoRA underfits in-domain, so method-only averages can obscure when LoRA becomes competitive. Supporting rollout, patch-to-patch, and CKA analyses are directionally consistent with the controlled matrix. Overall, matched-learning-rate evaluation materially changes the interpretation of Full FT versus LoRA, and attention drift is most useful as a descriptive diagnostic of representation preservation rather than a causal explanation of transfer behavior.

</details>


### [67] [Shifting the Gradient: Understanding How Defensive Training Methods Protect Language Model Integrity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16423)
*Satchel Grant,Victor Gillioz,Jake Ward,Thomas McGrath*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究比较了两种防御性训练方法PPS和IP，发现它们虽然都能防止LLM获得特定特质（如"邪恶性"），但通过不同的机制实现防御效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PPS和IP方法都能通过向LLM添加特质诱导对象来防御特质获取，但它们的相似过程和惊人效果引发了一个问题：这两种方法是否在做同样的事情？研究者希望通过行为学和机制比较来揭示它们的异同。

Method: 使用"邪恶性"作为案例研究特质，通过行为比较和机制分析来研究PPS和IP。行为上分析防御效果和减少已有特质表达的能力；机制上分析激活梯度变化、余弦相似性分析和下一个令牌预测损失。

Result: PPS和IP通过不同机制实现防御：PPS通过沿PPS向量轴将激活梯度转向衰减方向，当与特质表达轴对齐时可以逆转梯度压力；IP则具有不同的梯度特征，更分散，并通过"解释掉"训练数据中的特质表达来减少下一个令牌预测损失。

Conclusion: PPS和IP虽然都能防御特质获取，但工作机制不同：PPS能防御特质获取并主动减少已有表达，而IP对已有特质表达模型无效。IP的机制仍不完全清楚，需要进一步研究。

Abstract: Defensive training methods such as positive preventative steering (PPS) and inoculation prompting (IP) offer surprising results through seemingly similar processes: both add trait-inducing objects to large language models (LLMs) during training, and both defend the LLM against acquiring the trait. The surprising success of these methods comes with the question: how do they work? Are PPS and IP doing the same thing? We provide behavioral and mechanistic comparisons of these two methods using "evilness" as a case-study trait. Our central finding is that PPS and IP achieve their defensive benefits through distinct mechanisms. Behaviorally, we show that neither PPS nor IP operates through a purely associative mechanism; and PPS can both defend against trait acquisition and actively reduce pre-existing expression, whereas IP is ineffective in models that were previously finetuned to express the trait. This behavioral divergence is reflected mechanistically: PPS shifts the activation gradient towards an attenuating direction along the PPS vector axis. When the PPS vector is aligned with a trait-expressing axis, it can reverse the gradient pressure, reducing rather than increasing activation along that axis. In contrast, IP continues to resist a precise mechanistic account. Direct cosine similarity analyses reveal that IP has a characteristically different gradient signature than PPS, and qualitative analyses reveal IP's gradient to be more diffuse. Furthermore, IP reduces the next-token prediction loss on trait-expressing data where PPS need not, consistent with the notion that IP "explains away" the trait-expression in the training data. Taken together, our analyses reveal distinct mechanisms by which each method operates and highlight open questions about IP's mechanistic picture.

</details>


### [68] [Non-Stationarity in the Embedding Space of Time Series Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16428)
*Jinmyeong Choi,Brad Shook,Artur Dubrawski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了时间序列基础模型嵌入空间中非平稳性的可检测性问题，区分了分布非平稳性和时间非平稳性，并分析了不同模型对各类非平稳性的检测能力差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前时间序列基础模型作为通用特征提取器被广泛使用，但其嵌入空间中的非平稳性概念理解不足。现有研究常将非平稳性与分布漂移混为一谈，这模糊了经典时间序列分析和统计过程控制中长期存在的基本区别。在统计过程控制中，非平稳性标志着过程离开稳定状态，检测这种偏离对于质量监控和变点分析至关重要。

Method: 研究采用控制条件下的实验方法，分析不同形式的分布非平稳性（均值漂移、方差变化和线性趋势）在TSFM嵌入空间中的线性可访问性。同时考察时间非平稳性（由持续性引起，反映弱平稳性违反而非显式分布漂移）。通过扫描漂移强度并探测多个TSFM模型，评估嵌入空间对非平稳性的可检测性。

Result: 研究发现嵌入空间对非平稳性的可检测性平滑下降，不同模型表现出特定于模型的失效模式。不同TSFM在检测各类非平稳性方面存在显著差异，揭示了模型特定的局限性。

Conclusion: 该研究为理解时间序列基础模型嵌入空间中的非平稳性提供了诊断框架，强调了区分分布非平稳性和时间非平稳性的重要性，并揭示了不同模型在检测非平稳性方面的特定失效模式，对质量监控和变点分析应用具有指导意义。

Abstract: Time series foundation models (TSFMs) are widely used as generic feature extractors, yet the notion of non-stationarity in their embedding spaces remains poorly understood. Recent work often conflates non-stationarity with distribution shift, blurring distinctions fundamental to classical time-series analysis and long-standing methodologies such as statistical process control (SPC). In SPC, non-stationarity signals a process leaving a stable regime - via shifts in mean, variance, or emerging trends - and detecting such departures is central to quality monitoring and change-point analysis. Motivated by this diagnostic tradition, we study how different forms of distributional non-stationarity - mean shifts, variance changes, and linear trends - become linearly accessible in TSFM embedding spaces under controlled conditions. We further examine temporal non-stationarity arising from persistence, which reflects violations of weak stationarity due to long-memory or near-unit-root behavior rather than explicit distributional shifts. By sweeping shift strength and probing multiple TSFMs, we find that embedding-space detectability of non-stationarity degrades smoothly and that different models exhibit distinct, model-specific failure modes.

</details>


### [69] [(Sparse) Attention to the Details: Preserving Spectral Fidelity in ML-based Weather Forecasting Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16429)
*Maksim Zhdanov,Ana Lucic,Max Welling,Jan-Willem van de Meent*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Mosaic是一个概率天气预报模型，通过功能扰动和块稀疏注意力机制解决ML天气预报中的频谱退化问题，在1.5°分辨率下达到最先进水平，生成校准良好的集合预报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决基于机器学习的天气预报中两个主要的频谱退化来源：1)针对集合平均的确定性训练；2)压缩编码造成的信息瓶颈。

Method: 通过学习的功能扰动生成集合成员，使用块稀疏注意力机制在原生分辨率网格上操作，该机制通过跨空间相邻查询共享键和值，以线性成本捕获长程依赖关系。

Result: 在1.5°分辨率、2.14亿参数下，Mosaic匹配或优于在6倍更精细数据上训练的模型，在高层大气变量上达到最先进水平，生成校准良好的集合，其个体成员在所有解析频率上表现出近乎完美的频谱对齐。24个成员、10天预报在单个H100 GPU上耗时不到12秒。

Conclusion: Mosaic通过创新的功能扰动和硬件对齐的注意力机制，有效解决了ML天气预报中的频谱退化问题，在保持高效率的同时实现了高质量的集合预报。

Abstract: We introduce Mosaic, a probabilistic weather forecasting model that addresses two principal sources of spectral degradation in ML-based weather prediction: (1) deterministic training against ensemble means and (2) compressive encoding creating an information bottleneck. Mosaic generates ensemble members through learned functional perturbations and operates on native-resolution grids via block-sparse attention, a hardware-aligned mechanism that captures long-range dependencies at linear cost by sharing keys and values across spatially adjacent queries. At 1.5$°$ resolution with 214M parameters, Mosaic matches or outperforms models trained on 6 times finer data on headline upper-air variables and achieves state-of-the-art results among 1.5$°$ models, producing well-calibrated ensembles whose individual members exhibit near-perfect spectral alignment across all resolved frequencies. A 24-member, 10-day forecast takes under 12 seconds on a single H100 GPU.

</details>


### [70] [Sampling for Quality: Training-Free Reward-Guided LLM Decoding via Sequential Monte Carlo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16453)
*Jelena Markovic-Voronov,Wenhui Zhu,Bo Long,Zhipeng Wang,Suyash Gupta,Kayhan Behdin,Bee-Chung Chen,Deepak Agarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于概率框架的奖励引导解码方法，通过结合模型转移概率和前缀相关奖励潜力来定义序列级目标分布，无需训练模型权重，仅通过推理时采样提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准解码方法主要优化token级似然而非序列级质量，存在局限性。需要一种训练无关的方法，在推理时通过奖励引导提升序列生成质量。

Method: 建立概率框架，定义奖励增强的目标分布，结合模型转移概率和前缀相关奖励潜力。开发序列蒙特卡洛算法，包括计算高效的前缀变体和前瞻变体。集成重采样-移动更新和Metropolis-Hastings更新，支持块级生成。

Result: 在三个7B模型上验证：代码生成（HumanEval）提升达54.9%，超越最强基线9.1%-15.3%；数学推理（MATH500）提升达8.8%。Qwen2.5-7B在HumanEval达87.8%，MATH500达78.4%，持续超越GRPO强化学习方法。

Conclusion: 提出的训练无关奖励引导解码框架显著提升大语言模型在代码生成和数学推理任务上的性能，通过推理时采样实现序列级质量优化，超越了现有解码方法和强化学习方法。

Abstract: We introduce a principled probabilistic framework for reward-guided decoding in large language models, addressing the limitations of standard decoding methods that optimize token-level likelihood rather than sequence-level quality. Our method defines a reward-augmented target distribution over complete sequences by combining model transition probabilities with prefix-dependent reward potentials. Importantly, the approach is training-free: it leaves model weights unchanged and instead modifies the inference distribution via reward potentials, with all gains arising purely from inference-time sampling. To sample from this distribution, we develop Sequential Monte Carlo algorithms, including a computationally efficient prefix-only variant and a lookahead variant whose intermediate targets match the exact marginals of the full sequence distribution. The framework also integrates resample-move updates with Metropolis-Hastings rejuvenation and supports block-wise generation, subsuming common decoding strategies such as temperature sampling and power-tempered objectives. Empirical results across three 7B models show significant gains. On code generation (HumanEval), our method improves base performance by up to 54.9% and surpasses the strongest sampling baselines by 9.1%-15.3%. On mathematical reasoning (MATH500), it achieves gains of up to 8.8%. Notably, it reaches 87.8% on HumanEval and 78.4% on MATH500 with Qwen2.5-7B, consistently outperforming the reinforcement learning method GRPO.

</details>


### [71] [Positive-Only Drifting Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16519)
*Qi Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PODPO是一种免似然、免梯度裁剪的生成式在线强化学习方法，通过优势加权局部对比漂移进行策略更新，仅使用正优势样本来引导动作向高回报区域移动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统高斯策略和基于流的方法在在线强化学习中存在局限性：单峰表达能力受限、梯度裁剪复杂、信任区域要求严格，且都依赖后验惩罚负样本来纠正错误动作。

Method: PODPO利用漂移模型，通过优势加权局部对比漂移进行策略更新，仅使用正优势样本，利用生成模型的局部平滑性实现主动错误预防。

Result: 该方法为在线环境中的生成式策略学习开辟了新的有前景的途径。

Conclusion: PODPO提供了一种优雅的解决方案，能够引导动作向高回报区域移动，同时利用生成模型的局部平滑性实现主动错误预防。

Abstract: In the field of online reinforcement learning (RL), traditional Gaussian policies and flow-based methods are often constrained by their unimodal expressiveness, complex gradient clipping, or stringent trust-region requirements. Moreover, they all rely on post-hoc penalization of negative samples to correct erroneous actions. This paper introduces Positive-Only Drifting Policy Optimization (PODPO), a likelihood-free and gradient-clipping-free generative approach for online RL. By leveraging the drifting model, PODPO performs policy updates via advantage-weighted local contrastive drifting. Relying solely on positive-advantage samples, it elegantly steers actions toward high-return regions while exploiting the inherent local smoothness of the generative model to enable proactive error prevention. In doing so, PODPO opens a promising new pathway for generative policy learning in online settings.

</details>


### [72] [SCATR: Simple Calibrated Test-Time Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16535)
*Divya Shyamal,Marta Knežević,Lan Tran,Chanakya Ekbote,Vijay Lingam,Paul Pu Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SCATR是一种高效的测试时选择方法，通过少量校准数据学习轻量级评分器，在推理时从多个候选响应中选择最佳答案，相比传统置信度启发式方法提升达9%，且比LoRA微调节省大量计算资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时缩放（TTS）通过推理时分配额外计算来提升大语言模型性能，通常采用并行缩放生成多个候选响应并用Best-of-N策略选择最佳。现有方法中，学习型评分器（如PRM）效果好但训练和运行成本高，而基于token对数概率的轻量级置信度启发式方法虽然便宜但效果较差。需要一种既能提升轻量级方法效果又不增加学习型评分器高成本的新方法。

Method: SCATR方法从少量校准数据中学习轻量级评分器，利用基础模型的隐藏表示进行BoN排序。该方法通过校准集训练一个简单的评分函数，在推理时从多个候选响应中选择最佳答案，相比传统方法显著减少了可训练参数和计算需求。

Result: 在编程和数学推理基准测试中，SCATR相比先前的置信度基线方法提升达9%。与相同校准数据上的LoRA微调相比，达到相当准确率的同时减少了8000倍可训练参数，训练和推理延迟分别降低150倍和1000倍。与强PRM基线相比，在数学任务上提升达7.8%，编程任务上提升达4.2%，同时实现1000倍更快的推理速度。

Conclusion: SCATR为可扩展的测试时选择提供了强大的准确率-效率权衡，在保持高性能的同时大幅降低了计算成本，是测试时缩放的有效解决方案。

Abstract: Test-time scaling (TTS) improves large language models (LLMs) by allocating additional compute at inference time. In practice, TTS is often achieved through parallel scaling: generating multiple candidate responses and selecting the best via a Best-of-N (BoN) strategy. Its effectiveness therefore hinges on the scoring function. Learned scorers such as process reward models (PRMs) can be strong, but they are expensive to train and run. Lightweight confidence heuristics based on token log-probabilities are much cheaper, yet we find that they often perform substantially worse. To improve on lightweight confidence heuristics without incurring the full cost of stronger learned scorers, we introduce SCATR, a simple and efficient BoN ranking method that learns a lightweight scorer from a small calibration set using hidden representations from the base model. Across coding and mathematical reasoning benchmarks, SCATR improves over prior confidence-based baselines by up to 9%. Relative to LoRA fine-tuning on the same calibration data, it achieves comparable accuracy with up to 8000x fewer trainable parameters and much lower compute, reducing training and inference latency by up to 150x and 1000x, respectively. SCATR is also competitive with strong PRM baselines, and in several settings improves accuracy by up to 7.8% on math and 4.2% on coding while enabling up to 1000x faster inference. Overall, SCATR offers a strong accuracy-efficiency trade-off for scalable test-time selection.

</details>


### [73] [An Interpretable Framework Applying Protein Words to Predict Protein-Small Molecule Complementary Pairing Rules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16550)
*Jingke Chen,Jingrui Zhong,Tazneen Hossain Tani,Zidong Su,Xiaochun Zhang,Boxue Tian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PWRules是一个可解释的蛋白质-小分子结合预测框架，通过识别优势小分子片段并定义它们与蛋白质"单词"（语义序列单元）之间的互补配对规则，提供比黑盒深度学习模型更好的可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管深度学习模型在蛋白质-小分子结合预测中具有高准确性，但药物发现仍然依赖于蛋白质-配体相互作用原理和启发式方法。现有黑盒模型缺乏可解释性，限制了其在药物发现中的应用。

Method: 开发了PWRules框架：1）利用结合亲和力数据识别优势小分子片段；2）通过可解释性模块定义这些片段与蛋白质"单词"（语义序列单元）之间的互补配对规则；3）使用PWScore函数对规则进行排序以优先选择活性化合物。

Result: PWScore在基准数据集上表现优异，性能与基于物理的模型（Glide）和深度学习模型（PSICHIC）相当，对训练集外的蛋白质靶点（如SARS-CoV-2主要蛋白酶）具有广泛适用性。与现有方法集成时显示出优越的富集性能。结构分析表明，学习的单词-片段规则在配体结合口袋附近显著富集。

Conclusion: 通过提取和应用互补配对规则，PWRules为药物发现提供了一个可解释的框架，能够捕获互补相互作用信息，并在没有明确结构指导的情况下学习到与结构相关的规则。

Abstract: Despite the high accuracy of 'black box' deep learning models, drug discovery still relies on protein-ligand interaction principles and heuristics. To improve interpretability of protein-small molecule binding predictions, we developed the PWRules framework, which applies binding affinity data to identify privileged small molecule fragments and subsequently defines complementary pairing rules between these fragments and protein words (semantic sequence units) through an interpretability module. The resulting word-fragment rules are then ranked by the PWScore function to prioritize active compounds. Evaluations on benchmark datasets show that PWScore achieves competitive performance comparable to the physics-based model (Glide) and the deep learning model (PSICHIC) and shows broad applicability for protein targets outside the training dataset, e.g., SARS-CoV-2 main protease. Notably, PWScore captures complementary interaction information, yielding superior enrichment performance when integrated with these established methods. Structural analysis of protein-ligand complexes indicates that learned word-fragment rules are significantly enriched near ligand-binding pockets, despite training without explicit structural guidance. By extracting and applying complementary pairing rules, PWRules provides an interpretable framework for drug discovery.

</details>


### [74] [LLM as a Tool, Not an Agent: Code-Mined Tree Transformations for Neural Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16555)
*Masakazu Yoshimura,Zitang Sun,Yuiko Sakuma,Junji Otsuka,Atsushi Irie,Takeshi Ohashi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LLMasTool：一种基于分层树的神经架构搜索框架，结合算法搜索与LLM辅助，实现稳定且开放式的模型演化，超越传统NAS方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统算法驱动的NAS依赖人工设计的搜索空间限制了开放式探索，而基于LLM的编码方法又存在生成复杂有效架构不可靠、偏向训练数据中有限模式的问题。需要结合可靠算法搜索与强大LLM辅助的优势。

Method: 提出LLMasTool分层树基NAS框架：1) 从任意源代码自动提取可重用模块；2) 将完整架构表示为分层树；3) 通过可靠的树变换而非代码生成进行演化；4) 粗粒度规划采用多样性引导算法和贝叶斯建模提高探索效率；5) LLM解决剩余自由度确保有意义的演化轨迹和可执行架构。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100和ImageNet16-120数据集上，分别比现有NAS方法提升0.69、1.83和2.68个百分点，证明了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: LLMasTool通过结合算法可靠性与LLM灵活性，实现了稳定且开放式的神经架构演化，超越了传统NAS方法和完全基于LLM的方法，能够探索超出LLM固有偏见的多样化方向。

Abstract: Neural Architecture Search (NAS) aims to automatically discover high-performing deep neural network (DNN) architectures. However, conventional algorithm-driven NAS relies on carefully hand-crafted search spaces to ensure executability, which restricts open-ended exploration. Recent coding-based agentic approaches using large language models (LLMs) reduce manual design, but current LLMs struggle to reliably generate complex, valid architectures, and their proposals are often biased toward a narrow set of patterns observed in their training data. To bridge reliable algorithmic search with powerful LLM assistance, we propose LLMasTool, a hierarchical tree-based NAS framework for stable and open-ended model evolution. Our method automatically extracts reusable modules from arbitrary source code and represents full architectures as hierarchical trees, enabling evolution through reliable tree transformations rather than code generation. At each evolution step, coarse-level planning is governed by a diversity-guided algorithm that leverages Bayesian modeling to improve exploration efficiency, while the LLM resolves the remaining degrees of freedom to ensure a meaningful evolutionary trajectory and an executable generated architecture. With this formulation, instead of fully agentic LLM approaches, our method explores diverse directions beyond the inherent biases in the LLM. Our method improves over existing NAS methods by 0.69, 1.83, and 2.68 points on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and ImageNet16-120, demonstrating its effectiveness.

</details>


### [75] [S-GRPO: Unified Post-Training for Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16557)
*Yuming Yan,Kai Tang,Sihong Chen,Ke Xu,Dan Hu,Qun Yu,Pengfei Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: S-GRPO是一个统一的后训练框架，将模仿学习指导与偏好优化的多轨迹探索相结合，通过条件真实轨迹注入机制解决SFT和RL在视觉任务中的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大型视觉语言模型的后训练方法主要分为监督微调(SFT)和强化学习(RL)两种范式，但两者在孤立应用时都存在效率问题。SFT强制模型沿单一专家轨迹生成，常因分布偏移导致灾难性遗忘；RL探索多轨迹但在稀疏奖励的视觉任务中常遭遇优化崩溃（冷启动问题）。

Method: 提出监督组相对策略优化(S-GRPO)，为直接生成视觉任务设计条件真实轨迹注入(CGI)机制。当二元验证器检测到采样轨迹组中完全探索失败时，CGI将已验证的真实轨迹注入候选池，并赋予确定性最大奖励，在组相对优势估计中强制执行正信号，将监督学习目标重新表述为策略梯度的高优势组件。

Result: 理论分析和实证结果表明，S-GRPO优雅地弥合了SFT和RL之间的差距，大幅加速收敛，在实现优越领域适应的同时保留了基础模型的通用能力。

Conclusion: S-GRPO通过将模仿学习指导整合到偏好优化的多轨迹探索中，解决了当前后训练方法的局限性，使模型能够动态平衡利用专家轨迹和探索新视觉概念。

Abstract: Current post-training methodologies for adapting Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) generally fall into two paradigms: Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) and Reinforcement Learning (RL). Despite their prevalence, both approaches suffer from inefficiencies when applied in isolation. SFT forces the model's generation along a single expert trajectory, often inducing catastrophic forgetting of general multimodal capabilities due to distributional shifts. Conversely, RL explores multiple generated trajectories but frequently encounters optimization collapse - a cold-start problem where an unaligned model fails to spontaneously sample any domain-valid trajectories in sparse-reward visual tasks. In this paper, we propose Supervised Group Relative Policy Optimization (S-GRPO), a unified post-training framework that integrates the guidance of imitation learning into the multi-trajectory exploration of preference optimization. Tailored for direct-generation visual tasks, S-GRPO introduces Conditional Ground-Truth Trajectory Injection (CGI). When a binary verifier detects a complete exploratory failure within a sampled group of trajectories, CGI injects the verified ground-truth trajectory into the candidate pool. By assigning a deterministic maximal reward to this injected anchor, S-GRPO enforces a positive signal within the group-relative advantage estimation. This mechanism reformulates the supervised learning objective as a high-advantage component of the policy gradient, compelling the model to dynamically balance between exploiting the expert trajectory and exploring novel visual concepts. Theoretical analysis and empirical results demonstrate that S-GRPO gracefully bridges the gap between SFT and RL, drastically accelerates convergence, and achieves superior domain adaptation while preserving the base model's general-purpose capabilities.

</details>


### [76] [Cross-Modal Generation: From Commodity WiFi to High-Fidelity mmWave and RFID Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16558)
*Zhixiong Yang,Long Jing,Yao Li,Shuli Cheng,Guoxuan Chi,Chenyu Wen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RF-CMG：基于扩散模型的跨模态生成方法，利用数据丰富的WiFi信号合成稀缺模态（毫米波和RFID）的高保真RF数据，解决无线领域数据不平衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线领域存在显著的数据不平衡问题：WiFi数据丰富，但毫米波和RFID数据稀缺且获取成本高，这导致难以训练高质量的数据稀缺模态生成模型。

Method: 提出RF-CMG方法，将跨模态生成解耦为高频引导和低频约束。引入模态引导嵌入（MGE）模块引导反向扩散轨迹朝向目标高频分布，以及低频模态一致性（LFMC）模块逐步强制执行低频约束以抑制源模态结构偏差积累。

Result: 与多种主流生成模型相比，RF-CMG在合成RFID和毫米波信号方面表现出优越性能。生成的数据在手势识别任务中有效，并分析了合成数据比例对下游性能的影响。

Conclusion: RF-CMG能够有效利用数据丰富的WiFi信号生成高质量的数据稀缺模态RF数据，为解决无线领域数据不平衡问题提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: AIGC has shown remarkable success in CV and NLP, and has recently demonstrated promising potential in the wireless domain. However, significant data imbalance exists across RF modalities, with abundant WiFi data but scarce mmWave and RFID data due to high acquisition cost. This makes it difficult to train high-quality generative models for these data-scarce modalities. In this work, we propose RF-CMG, a diffusion-based cross-modal generative method that leverages data-rich WiFi signals to synthesize high-fidelity RF data for scarce modalities including mmWave and RFID. The key insight of RF-CMG is to decouple cross-modal generation into high-frequency guidance and low-frequency constraint, which respectively learn high-frequency distribution from limited target modality data and preserve the underlying physical structure via low-frequency constraints during generation. On this basis, we introduce a Modality-Guided Embedding (MGE) module to steer the reverse diffusion trajectory toward the target high-frequency distribution, and a Low-Frequency Modality Consistency (LFMC) module to progressively enforce low-frequency constraints to suppress the accumulation of source-modality structural biases during inference, enabling high-quality target-modality generation. Performance comparison with several prevalent generative models demonstrates that RF-CMG achieves superior performance in synthesizing RFID and mmWave signals. We further showcase the effectiveness of the data generated by RF-CMG in gesture recognition tasks, and analyze the impact of the proportion of synthetic data on downstream performance.

</details>


### [77] [Reasoning on the Manifold: Bidirectional Consistency for Self-Verification in Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16565)
*Jiaoyang Ruan,Xin Gao,Yinda Chen,Hengyu Zeng,Liang Du,Guanghao Li,Jie Fu,Jian Pu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出BMC方法，通过几何视角验证扩散大语言模型的推理正确性，无需训练即可评估推理轨迹的稳定性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型在全局规划方面有结构优势，但验证其通过有效推理轨迹得到正确答案仍是一个关键挑战

Method: 提出几何视角：流形上的推理。引入双向流形一致性（BMC），这是一种无需训练的无监督度量，通过前向掩码和后向重建循环量化生成序列的稳定性

Result: BMC在完整推理生命周期中表现出多功能性：1）诊断中作为无需真实答案的解决方案有效性判别器；2）推理中支持拒绝重采样以集中计算资源；3）对齐中作为密集几何奖励，将稀疏结果监督转化为细粒度指导

Conclusion: 内在几何稳定性是dLLMs正确性的稳健指标，BMC方法为验证扩散大语言模型的推理正确性提供了有效工具

Abstract: While Diffusion Large Language Models (dLLMs) offer structural advantages for global planning, efficiently verifying that they arrive at correct answers via valid reasoning traces remains a critical challenge. In this work, we propose a geometric perspective: Reasoning on the Manifold. We hypothesize that valid generation trajectories reside as stable attractors on the high-density manifold of the learned distribution, whereas invalid paths exhibit off-manifold drift.
