To operationalize this, we introduce Bidirectional Manifold Consistency (BMC), a training-free, unsupervised metric that quantifies the stability of the generated sequence through a forward-masking and backward-reconstruction cycle. Empirically, we demonstrate BMC's versatility across the full reasoning lifecycle: (1) in Diagnosis, it serves as a robust discriminator of solution validity without ground truth answer; (2) in Inference, it enables rejection resampling to effectively concentrate computational resources on complex reasoning tasks; and (3) in Alignment, it functions as a dense geometric reward that transforms sparse outcome supervision into fine-grained guidance, empowering models to self-evolve beyond standard baselines. Our results establish intrinsic geometric stability as a robust indicator of correctness for dLLMs.

</details>


### [78] [In Search of Lost DNA Sequence Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16570)
*Zhijiang Tang,Jiaxin Qi,Yan Cui,Jinli Ou,Yuhua Zheng,Jianqiang Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对DNA序列预训练中的三个关键但被忽视的问题（不合适的下游数据集、邻居掩码策略的内在缺陷、词汇表缺乏详细讨论）进行了全面研究，提出了原则性指导方针，并通过实验验证了这些问题的重要性，最后引入了标准化测试平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大规模DNA序列预训练取得了显著进展，但现有研究过度强调预训练规模和自定义下游评估数据集，而忽视了预训练范式中一些基本组件。本文揭示了DNA预训练中三个关键但被忽视的问题。

Method: 通过全面调查提出原则性指导方针，包括评估数据集的选择标准、指导任务设计以及深入的词汇表分析。进行大量实验验证所识别问题的重要性，并引入标准化测试平台以实现DNA预训练方法的可重复和严格基准测试。

Result: 大量实验验证了所识别问题的重要性，并支持了建议背后的原理。提出的指导方针有助于解决DNA预训练中的关键缺陷。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了DNA预训练中的三个关键问题，提出了相应的解决方案和指导方针，并引入了标准化测试平台，以促进基因组基础模型的开发。

Abstract: DNA sequence encoding is fundamental to gene function prediction, protein synthesis, and diverse downstream biological tasks. Despite the substantial progress achieved by large-scale DNA sequence pretraining, existing studies have overwhelmingly emphasized pretraining scale and custom downstream evaluation datasets, while neglecting some essential components of the pretraining paradigm. In this paper, we reveal three critical yet heretofore overlooked problems in DNA pretraining: inappropriate downstream datasets, inherent flaws in the neighbor-masking strategy, and the lack of detailed discussion on vocabulary. Therefore, we undertake comprehensive investigations and propose principled guidelines, including selection criteria for evaluation datasets, guiding task design, and in-depth vocabulary analysis. Extensive experiments validate the significance of our identified problems and support the rationale behind our recommendations. Finally, we introduce a standardized testbed that enables reproducible and rigorous benchmarking of DNA pretraining methods to advance the development of genomic foundation models.

</details>


### [79] [FedOBP: Federated Optimal Brain Personalization through Cloud-Edge Element-wise Decoupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16574)
*Xingyan Chen,Tian Du,Changqiao Xu,Fuzhen Zhuang,Lujie Zhong,Gabriel-Miro Muntean,Enmao Diao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedOBP提出了一种联邦最优大脑个性化算法，通过量化阈值机制和元素级重要性评分，将经典剪枝理论与联邦参数解耦相结合，在保持少量个性化参数的同时提升模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习面临客户端数据异构性和资源受限移动设备的挑战，个性化联邦学习通过将共享全局知识适应本地数据分布来解决这一问题。模型解耦方法将模型分为全局和个性化参数，但关键问题是如何选择哪些参数应该个性化以平衡全局知识共享和本地适应。

Method: 提出FedOBP算法，包含量化阈值机制和元素级重要性评分。该评分扩展了最优大脑损伤剪枝理论，通过纳入泰勒展开中一阶导数的联邦近似来评估每个参数对个性化的重要性。将原本在客户端执行的度量计算转移到服务器端，减轻资源受限移动设备的负担。

Result: 在多种数据集和异构场景下的广泛实验表明，FedOBP优于最先进的方法，同时只需要个性化极少量的参数。

Conclusion: 这是第一个将经典基于显著性的剪枝理论与联邦参数解耦相结合的工作，为基于参数对本地损失景观敏感性选择个性化参数提供了严格的理论依据。FedOBP在保持高性能的同时显著减少了需要个性化的参数数量。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) faces challenges from client data heterogeneity and resource-constrained mobile devices, which can degrade model accuracy. Personalized Federated Learning (PFL) addresses this issue by adapting shared global knowledge to local data distributions. A promising approach in PFL is model decoupling, which separates the model into global and personalized parameters, raising the key question of which parameters should be personalized to balance global knowledge sharing and local adaptation. In this paper, we propose a Federated Optimal Brain Personalization (FedOBP) algorithm with a quantile-based thresholding mechanism and introduce an element-wise importance score. This score extends Optimal Brain Damage (OBD) pruning theory by incorporating a federated approximation of the first-order derivative in the Taylor expansion to evaluate the importance of each parameter for personalization. Moreover, we move the metric computation originally performed on clients to the server side, to alleviate the burden on resource-constrained mobile devices. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to bridge classical saliency-based pruning theory with federated parameter decoupling, providing a rigorous theoretical justification for selecting personalized parameters based on their sensitivity to local loss landscapes. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FedOBP outperforms state-of-the-art methods across diverse datasets and heterogeneity scenarios, while requiring personalization of only a very small number of personalized parameters.

</details>


### [80] [Evaluating Temporal and Structural Anomaly Detection Paradigms for DDoS Traffic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16575)
*Yasmin Souza Lima,Rodrigo Moreira,Larissa F. Rodrigues Moreira,Tereza Cristina M. de B. Carvalho,Flávio de Oliveira Silva*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出轻量级决策框架，通过诊断指标优先选择时域或结构特征进行DDoS异常检测，避免固定特征表示


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督异常检测方法通常固定使用时域或结构特征，但未验证哪种特征空间最适合数据，导致可能选择次优特征表示

Method: 提出轻量级决策框架，使用两个诊断指标：聚合流信号的滞后1自相关性和PCA累计解释方差，优先选择时域或结构特征；诊断不确定时保留混合选项作为未来备选方案

Result: 在两个统计特性不同的数据集上实验表明，结构特征始终匹配或优于时域特征，当时域依赖性减弱时性能差距扩大

Conclusion: 结构特征在DDoS异常检测中通常更有效，提出的决策框架能指导特征选择，避免固定特征表示的局限性

Abstract: Unsupervised anomaly detection is widely used to detect Distributed Denial-of-Service (DDoS) attacks in cloud-native 5G networks, yet most studies assume a fixed traffic representation, either temporal or structural, without validating which feature space best matches the data. We propose a lightweight decision framework that prioritizes temporal or structural features before training, using two diagnostics: lag-1 autocorrelation of an aggregated flow signal and PCA cumulative explained variance. When the probes are inconclusive, the framework reserves a hybrid option as a future fallback rather than an empirically validated branch. Experiments on two statistically distinct datasets with Isolation Forest, One-Class SVM, and KMeans show that structural features consistently match or outperform temporal ones, with the performance gap widening as temporal dependence weakens.

</details>


### [81] [Towards Trustworthy Depression Estimation via Disentangled Evidential Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16579)
*Fangyuan Liu,Sirui Zhao,Zeyu Zhang,Jinyang Huang,Feng-Qi Cui,Bin Luo,Tong Xu,Meng Li,Enhong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EviDep是一个基于证据学习的抑郁症自动评估框架，通过Normal-Inverse-Gamma分布联合量化抑郁严重程度及不确定度，采用频率感知特征提取和分离证据学习策略，有效减少跨模态冗余，提高临床筛查的鲁棒性和可信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中抑郁症自动评估易受信号损坏和环境噪声影响，现有确定性方法产生未校准的点估计，使临床系统面临过度自信误诊的风险。需要建立高度弹性和可信赖的评估范式。

Method: 提出EviDep证据学习框架：1) 频率感知特征提取模块使用基于小波的混合专家模型动态隔离任务无关噪声；2) 分离证据学习策略将共享共识与模态特定细节分离；3) 通过Normal-Inverse-Gamma分布联合量化抑郁严重程度及不确定度；4) 在贝叶斯融合前显式解耦表示，系统缓解证据冗余。

Result: 在AVEC 2013、2014、DAIC-WOZ和E-DAIC数据集上的广泛实验表明，EviDep实现了最先进的预测准确性和优越的不确定度校准，为可信赖的临床筛查提供了鲁棒的故障安全机制。

Conclusion: EviDep通过证据学习框架有效解决了多模态融合中的冗余问题，建立了高度弹性和可信赖的抑郁症评估范式，为临床筛查提供了鲁棒的故障安全机制。

Abstract: Automated depression estimation is highly vulnerable to signal corruption and ambient noise in real-world deployment. Prevailing deterministic methods produce uncalibrated point estimates, exposing safety-critical clinical systems to the severe risk of overconfident misdiagnoses. To establish a highly resilient and trustworthy assessment paradigm, we propose EviDep, an evidential learning framework that jointly quantifies depression severity alongside aleatoric and epistemic uncertainties via a Normal-Inverse-Gamma distribution. A fundamental vulnerability in multimodal evidential fusion is the uncontrolled accumulation of cross-modal redundancies. This structural flaw artificially inflates diagnostic confidence by double-counting overlapping evidence. To guarantee robust evidence synthesis, EviDep enforces strict information integrity. First, a Frequency-aware Feature Extraction module leverages a wavelet-based Mixture-of-Experts to dynamically isolate task-irrelevant noise, preserving the fidelity of diagnostic signals. Subsequently, a Disentangled Evidential Learning strategy separates the shared consensus from modality-specific nuances. By explicitly decoupling these representations before Bayesian fusion, EviDep systematically mitigates evidence redundancy. Extensive experiments on AVEC 2013, 2014, DAIC-WOZ, and E-DAIC confirm that EviDep achieves state-of-the-art predictive accuracy and superior uncertainty calibration, delivering a robust fail-safe mechanism for trustworthy clinical screening.

</details>


### [82] [Continuous ageing trajectory representations for knee-aware lifetime prediction of lithium-ion batteries across heterogeneous dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16580)
*Agnieszka Pregowska,Stefan Marynowicz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于连续表示的电池老化分析框架，从异构数据集中学习电压-容量和容量-循环轨迹，实现降解描述符的一致提取和早期寿命预测


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 锂离子电池老化评估面临电池间变异性、异构循环协议和数据驱动模型跨数据集可迁移性有限等挑战，特别是降解转变点（如拐点）的鲁棒识别和剩余使用寿命的早期预测仍是开放问题

Method: 基于连续表示的统一框架，从NASA、CALCE、ISU-ILCC等异构公共数据集中学习电压-容量和容量-循环轨迹的连续表示，实现降解描述符（曲率、平台长度、拐点相关指标）的一致提取

Result: 在250多个电池中观察到拐点起始与寿命终止之间的显著统计相关性（Pearson 0.75-0.84）；早期寿命模型在观测5-20个循环后即出现有意义的预测性能，且跨数据集域偏移下保持鲁棒

Conclusion: 该框架整合连续建模、特征提取和不确定性感知预测，提供可解释且数据集一致的方法，相比传统离散或基于特征的方法，降低了对采样分辨率的敏感性并提高了跨数据集一致性

Abstract: Accurate assessment of lithium-ion battery ageing is challenged by cell-to-cell variability, heterogeneous cycling protocols, and limited transferability of data-driven models across datasets. In particular, robust identification of degradation transitions, such as the knee point, and reliable early-life prediction of remaining useful life (RUL) remain open problems. This study proposes a unified framework for battery ageing analysis based on continuous representations of voltage-capacity and capacity-cycle trajectories learned from heterogeneous public datasets (NASA, CALCE, ISU-ILCC). The continuous formulation enables consistent extraction of degradation descriptors, including curvature, plateau length and knee-related metrics, while reducing sensitivity to dataset-specific discretisation. Across more than 250 cells, statistically significant correlations between knee onset and end-of-life (Pearson 0.75-0.84) are observed. Additional early-life analysis confirms that knee-related features retain predictive value when estimated from partial trajectories. Early-life models provide increasingly stable RUL predictions as the number of observed cycles increases, with meaningful predictive performance emerging within the first 5-20 cycles and remain robust under cross-dataset domain shift. The framework integrates continuous modelling, feature extraction and uncertainty-aware prediction, providing an interpretable and dataset-consistent approach demonstrating robustness across heterogeneous dataset types. Compared with conventional discrete or feature-based methods, the proposed representation reduces sensitivity to sampling resolution and improves cross-dataset consistency. The study is limited to laboratory-scale datasets and capacity-based end-of-life definitions.

</details>


### [83] [NCO4CVRP: Neural Combinatorial Optimization for the Capacitated Vehicle Routing Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16581)
*Mahir Labib Dihan,Md. Ashrafur Rahman Khan,Wasif Jalal,Md. Roqunuzzaman Sojib,Mashroor Hasan Bhuiyan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究改进了神经组合优化的推理技术，通过整合模拟退火和波束搜索等策略，显著提升了CVRP问题的求解质量和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经组合优化模型的推理技术存在局限性，容易陷入局部最优且缺乏多样性探索，需要改进推理策略以提升解决方案质量和模型泛化能力。

Method: 1. 将模拟退火整合到LEHD模型的随机重构方法中，引入概率接受机制；2. 在POMO方法中集成波束搜索，系统探索多个有前景的解决方案；3. 研究不同的推理策略（Softmax采样、贪婪、Gumbel-Softmax、Epsilon-Greedy）；4. 探索实例增强技术（水平和垂直翻转、旋转增强）。

Result: 实验表明这些改进显著减少了各种CVRP基准测试的最优性差距，波束搜索和基于模拟退火的随机重构方法始终表现出优越性能。

Conclusion: 通过改进推理技术和利用增强的搜索策略，该研究提升了神经组合优化模型在现实世界组合优化任务中的适用性和性能。

Abstract: Neural Combinatorial Optimization (NCO) has emerged as a powerful framework for solving combinatorial optimization problems by integrating deep learning-based models. This work focuses on improving existing inference techniques to enhance solution quality and generalization. Specifically, we modify the Random Re-Construct (RRC) approach of the Light Encoder Heavy Decoder (LEHD) model by incorporating Simulated Annealing (SA). Unlike the conventional RRC, which greedily replaces suboptimal segments, our SA-based modification introduces a probabilistic acceptance mechanism that allows the model to escape local optima and explore a more diverse solution space. Additionally, we enhance the Policy Optimization with Multiple Optima (POMO) approach by integrating Beam Search, enabling systematic exploration of multiple promising solutions while maintaining diversity in the search space. We further investigate different inference strategies, including Softmax Sampling, Greedy, Gumbel-Softmax, and Epsilon-Greedy, analyzing their impact on solution quality. Furthermore, we explore instance augmentation techniques, such as horizontal and vertical flipping and rotation-based augmentations, to improve model generalization across different CVRP instances. Our extensive experiments demonstrate that these modifications significantly reduce the optimality gap across various Capacitated Vehicle Routing Problem (CVRP) benchmarks, with Beam Search and SA-based RRC consistently yielding superior performance. By refining inference techniques and leveraging enhanced search strategies, our work contributes to the broader applicability of NCO models in real-world combinatorial optimization tasks.

</details>


### [84] [POLAR: Online Learning for LoRA Adapter Caching and Routing in Edge LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16583)
*Shaoang Li,Jian Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出POLAR框架，解决边缘部署大语言模型时LoRA适配器的缓存与路由联合优化问题，通过两时间尺度的上下文赌博机方法平衡探索与利用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘部署LLMs依赖轻量级LoRA适配器库，但GPU/DRAM只能缓存少量适配器。服务请求时若适配器不在缓存中需要从存储加载，产生显著延迟。这形成了两时间尺度的在线控制问题：慢时间尺度选择缓存哪些适配器，快时间尺度路由请求到未知效用的适配器。

Method: 提出POLAR框架，将联合缓存路由问题建模为两时间尺度上下文赌博机。包含缓存感知的LinUCB路由器和基于周期的缓存控制器。研究两个变体：固定周期版本提供鲁棒基线，具有任意上下文下的最坏情况遗憾保证；周期加倍版本POLAR+增加强制探索和改进的缓存优化。

Result: POLAR+在随机规律性和可缓存性条件下实现$\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(d\sqrt{NT}+\sqrt{KT})$次线性遗憾，其中路由项匹配标准上下文赌博机速率（对数因子内），表明内存层次结构不会从根本上减慢路由学习。使用15个真实LoRA适配器和实测GPU分页延迟的实验显示，自适应缓存控制显著优于非自适应基线，且缩放趋势与理论一致。

Conclusion: POLAR框架有效解决了边缘LLM部署中的适配器缓存路由联合优化问题，理论分析表明内存层次结构不会阻碍路由学习，实验验证了自适应缓存控制的优越性能。

Abstract: Edge deployment of large language models (LLMs) increasingly relies on libraries of lightweight LoRA adapters, yet GPU/DRAM can keep only a small resident subset at a time. Serving a request through a non-resident adapter requires paging its weights from storage, incurring measurable latency. This creates a two-timescale online control problem: on a slow timescale, the system selects which adapters remain resident in fast memory, while on a fast timescale it routes each request to an adapter whose context-dependent utility is unknown a priori. The two decisions are tightly coupled: the cache determines the cost of exploration, and the router determines which adapters receive informative feedback. We formulate this joint caching-and-routing problem as a two-timescale contextual bandit and propose POLAR (Paging and Online Learning for Adapter Routing). POLAR pairs a cache-aware LinUCB router with an epoch-based cache controller. We study two variants. A fixed-epoch version provides a robust baseline with worst-case regret guarantees under arbitrary contexts. An epoch-doubling version, POLAR+, adds forced exploration and improved cache optimization to achieve $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(d\sqrt{NT}+\sqrt{KT})$ sublinear regret under stochastic regularity and cacheability conditions, where $N$ is the adapter count, $K$ the cache size, $d$ the context dimension, and $T$ the horizon. The routing term matches the standard contextual-bandit rate up to logarithmic factors, showing that the memory hierarchy does not fundamentally slow routing learning. Experiments using 15 real LoRA adapters for Qwen2.5-7B together with measured GPU paging latencies show that adaptive cache control substantially outperforms non-adaptive baselines and exhibits scaling trends consistent with the theory.

</details>


### [85] [A Systematic Survey and Benchmark of Deep Learning for Molecular Property Prediction in the Foundation Model Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16586)
*Zongru Li,Xingsheng Chen,Honggang Wen,Regina Qianru Zhang,Ming Li,Xiaojin Zhang,Hongzhi Yin,Qiang Yang,Kwok-Yan Lam,Pietro Lio,Siu-Ming Yiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 这篇综述论文系统梳理了分子性质预测的四大范式，建立了统一分类体系，分析了当前基准测试的局限性，并提出了三个前瞻性发展方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子性质预测是连接分子结构与物理化学、生物学行为的关键技术，但当前领域存在数据标准化不足、基准测试方法不统一、可重复性差等问题，需要系统性的梳理和现代化改进。

Method: 论文采用四范式分类法（量子方法、描述符机器学习、几何深度学习、基础模型），建立统一分类体系，整合广泛使用的数据集和反映工业视角的数据集进行基准分析，考察数据整理、划分策略和评估协议的标准。

Result: 分析揭示了当前基准测试的挑战：立体化学不一致、异质检测来源、随机或定义不清的数据划分导致可重复性限制。这些发现促使基准设计向更透明、时间感知和骨架感知的方法现代化。

Conclusion: 提出了三个前瞻方向：1）嵌入量子一致性的物理感知学习；2）用于可信推理的不确定性校准基础模型；3）整合计算与实验数据的现实多模态基准生态系统。论文还提供了开源代码库。

Abstract: Molecular property prediction integrates quantum chemistry, cheminformatics, and deep learning to connect molecular structure with physicochemical and biological behavior. This survey traces four complementary paradigms, including Quantum, Descriptor Machine Learning, Geometric Deep Learning, and Foundation Models, and outlines a unified taxonomy linking molecular representations, model architectures, and interdisciplinary applications. Benchmark analyses integrate evidence from both widely used datasets and datasets reflecting industry perspectives, encompassing quantum, physicochemical, physiological, and biophysical domains. The survey examines current standards in data curation, splitting strategies, and evaluation protocols, highlighting challenges including inconsistent stereochemistry, heterogeneous assay sources, and reproducibility limitations under random or poorly defined splits. These observations motivate the modernization of benchmark design toward more transparent, time- and scaffold-aware methodologies. We further propose three forward-looking directions: (i) physics-aware learning embedding quantum consistency, (ii) uncertainty-calibrated foundation models for trustworthy inference, and (iii) realistic multimodal benchmark ecosystems integrating computational and experimental data. Repository: https://github.com/Zongru-Li/Survey-and-Benchmarks-of-DL-for-Molecular-Property-Prediction-in-the-Foundation-Model-Era.

</details>


### [86] [Hybrid Spectro-Temporal Fusion Framework for Structural Health Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16589)
*Jongyeop Kim,Jinki Kim,Doyun Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合到达时间间隔描述符和频谱特征的谱时对齐与融合框架，用于结构健康监测，显著优于传统方法，并在不同时间分辨率下优化了机器学习和深度学习模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构健康监测对确保工程系统安全至关重要，但传统方法在捕捉振动响应的精细和粗尺度动态方面存在局限，需要更全面的特征表示方法。

Method: 提出了谱时对齐框架和混合谱时融合框架，将到达时间间隔描述符与频谱特征相结合，捕捉振动响应的精细尺度和粗尺度动态。

Result: 在LDS V406电动振动台数据上的实验表明，提出的谱时表示显著优于传统输入形式。时间分辨率Δτ=0.02有利于传统机器学习模型，而Δτ=0.008能充分发挥深度学习架构的性能潜力。混合框架在准确性和稳定性方面均优于基线和对齐方法。

Conclusion: 提出的框架为基于振动的结构健康监测提供了鲁棒、准确和可靠的解决方案，通过谱时特征的融合显著提升了监测性能。

Abstract: Structural health monitoring plays a critical role in ensuring structural safety by analyzing vibration responses from engineering systems. This paper proposes a Spectro-Temporal Alignment framework and a Hybrid Spectro-Temporal Fusion framework that integrate arrival-time interval descriptors with spectral features to capture both fine-scale and coarse-scale vibration dynamics. Experiments conducted on data collected from an LDS V406 electrodynamic shaker demonstrate that the proposed spectro-temporal representations significantly outperform conventional input formulations. The results indicate that a temporal resolution (Δτ) of 0.008 of 0.02 favors traditional machine learning models, whereas a finer resolution (Δτ) of 0.008 effectively unlocks the performance potential of deep learning architectures. Beyond classification accuracy, a comprehensive stability analysis based on condensed indices, including mean performance, standard deviation, coefficient of variation, and balanced score, shows that the proposed hybrid framework consistently achieves higher accuracy with substantially lower variability compared to baseline and alignment-only approaches. Overall, these results demonstrate that the proposed framework provides a robust, accurate, and reliable solution for vibration-based structural health monitoring.

</details>


### [87] [Global Attention with Linear Complexity for Exascale Generative Data Assimilation in Earth System Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16590)
*Xiao Wang,Zezhong Zhang,Isaac Lyngaas,Hong-Jun Yoon,Jong-Youl Choi,Siming Liang,Janet Wang,Hristo G. Chipilski,Ashwin M. Aji,Feng Bao,Peter Jan van Leeuwen,Dan Lu,Guannan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的单阶段生成式数据同化框架STORM，通过贝叶斯后验采样替代传统预报-更新循环，在Frontier超计算机上实现了63%的强扩展效率和1.6 ExaFLOP的持续性能，能够处理200亿时空标记，实现公里级全球建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管百亿亿次计算显著推进了地球模拟，但地球系统状态的可扩展且准确的推断仍然是基本瓶颈，限制了不确定性量化和极端事件预测。传统数据同化方法在计算效率和可扩展性方面存在局限。

Method: 提出统一的单阶段生成式数据同化框架，将同化重新表述为贝叶斯后验采样，用计算密集、GPU高效的推断替代传统预报-更新循环。核心是STORM——一种具有全局注意力线性复杂度缩放算法的新型时空变换器，突破了二次注意力障碍。

Result: 在Frontier超级计算机的32,768个GPU上，该方法实现了63%的强扩展效率和1.6 ExaFLOP的持续性能。进一步扩展到200亿时空标记，实现了177k时间帧的公里级全球建模，达到了先前无法达到的规模。

Conclusion: 该方法建立了一个地球系统预测的新范式，能够处理先前无法达到的大规模时空建模，为准确天气和气候预测提供了突破性的解决方案。

Abstract: Accurate weather and climate prediction relies on data assimilation (DA), which estimates the Earth system state by integrating observations with models. While exascale computing has significantly advanced earth simulation, scalable and accurate inference of the Earth system state remains a fundamental bottleneck, limiting uncertainty quantification and prediction of extreme events. We introduce a unified one-stage generative DA framework that reformulates assimilation as Bayesian posterior sampling, replacing the conventional forecast-update cycle with compute-dense, GPU-efficient inference. At the core is STORM, a novel spatiotemporal transformer with a global attention linear-complexity scaling algorithm that breaks the quadratic attention barrier. On 32,768 GPUs of the Frontier supercomputer, our method achieves 63% strong scaling efficiency and 1.6 ExaFLOP sustained performance. We further scale to 20 billion spatiotemporal tokens, enabling km-scale global modeling over 177k temporal frames, regimes previously unreachable, establishing a new paradigm for Earth system prediction.

</details>


### [88] [Randomized Antipodal Search Done Right for Data Pareto Improvement of LLM Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16591)
*Ziwen Liu,Huawei Lin,Yide Ran,Denghui Zhang,Jianwen Xie,Chuan Li,Weijie Zhao,Zhaozhuo Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出RASLIK算法，通过随机化搜索改进LLM遗忘中的数据检索，在遗忘与保留间实现更好的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型有时会记忆不良知识，需要在部署后移除。现有遗忘方法假设遗忘集和保留集容易获取，但实践中遗忘通常由推理时的不良生成触发，使得相关数据检索成为核心挑战。

Method: 提出数据帕累托改进概念，并设计RASLIK检索算法，结合置换投影哈希与随机化反极搜索，降低选择方差，实现次线性复杂度。

Result: RASLIK在多个模型、数据集和遗忘算法中，一致优于确定性基线和甚至oracle采样，在质量和效率上实现双重增益。

Conclusion: 随机化搜索为数据中心的遗忘提供了原则性和可扩展的解决方案，通过改进数据检索扩展了遗忘与保留之间的可达成权衡边界。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) sometimes memorize undesirable knowledge, which must be removed after deployment. Prior work on machine unlearning has focused largely on optimization methods that adjust parameters to enforce forgetting while preserving retention. However, these approaches assume that the forget and retain sets are readily available, which rarely holds in practice. Unlearning is typically triggered by an undesired generation at inference time, making the retrieval of relevant data the central challenge.
  We introduce the notion of data Pareto improvement for LLM unlearning, which formalizes how retrieval can expand the achievable trade-off frontier between forgetting and retention. To realize this principle, we propose Randomized Antipodal Search on Linearized Influence Kernel (RASLIK), a retrieval algorithm that combines permutation-projection hashing with randomized antipodal search. RASLIK reduces selection variance, achieves sublinear complexity, and yields a double gain in both quality and efficiency. Across multiple models, datasets, and unlearning algorithms, RASLIK consistently outperforms deterministic baselines and even oracle sampling, establishing randomized search as a principled and scalable solution for data-centric unlearning.

</details>


### [89] [FedLLM: A Privacy-Preserving Federated Large Language Model for Explainable Traffic Flow Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16612)
*Seerat Kaur,Sukhjit Singh Sehra,Dariush Ebrahimi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedLLM是一个基于联邦学习和领域适应LLM的隐私保护分布式框架，用于可解释的多时间尺度短期交通流预测（15-60分钟）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交通预测方法存在局限性：大多数时空模型依赖集中式数据训练、使用数值表示、可解释性有限；近期LLM方法虽然提升了推理能力，但仍假设集中式数据可用，未能充分捕捉真实交通系统的分布式和异构特性。

Method: 提出FedLLM框架，包含四个关键贡献：1) 基于数据驱动的高速公路选择复合选择分数(CSS)；2) 在结构化交通提示上微调的领域适应LLM；3) 联邦学习框架，仅交换轻量级LoRA适配器参数；4) 支持上下文推理和跨区域泛化的结构化提示表示。

Result: 实验结果显示FedLLM在预测性能上优于集中式基线方法，同时产生结构化和可解释的输出，支持非独立同分布交通数据下的学习。

Conclusion: 联邦学习与领域适应LLM的结合为可扩展、隐私保护和可解释的交通预测提供了潜力，实现了分布式协作训练同时保持数据隐私。

Abstract: Traffic prediction plays a central role in intelligent transportation systems (ITS) by supporting real-time decision-making, congestion management, and long-term planning. However, many existing approaches face practical limitations. Most spatio-temporal models are trained on centralized data, rely on numerical representations, and offer limited explainability. Recent Large Language Model (LLM) methods improve reasoning capabilities but typically assume centralized data availability and do not fully capture the distributed and heterogeneous nature of real-world traffic systems. To address these challenges, this study proposes FedLLM (Federated LLM), a privacy-preserving and distributed framework for explainable multi-horizon short-term traffic flow prediction (15-60 minutes). The framework introduces four key contributions: 1) a Composite Selection Score (CSS) for data-driven freeway selection that captures structural diversity across traffic regions 2) a domain-adapted LLM fine-tuned on structured traffic prompts encoding spatial, temporal, and statistical context 3) FedLLM framework, that enables collaborative training across heterogeneous clients while exchanging only lightweight LoRA adapter parameters, 4) a structured prompt representation that supports contextual reasoning and cross-region generalization. The FedLLM design allows each client to learn from local traffic patterns while contributing to a shared global model through efficient parameter exchange, reducing communication overhead and keeping data private. This setup supports learning under non-IID traffic distributions. Experimental results show that FedLLM achieves improved predictive performance over centralized baselines, while producing structured and explainable outputs. These findings highlight the potential of combining FL with domain-adapted LLMs for scalable, privacy-aware, and explainable traffic prediction.

</details>


### [90] [FRIGID: Scaling Diffusion-Based Molecular Generation from Mass Spectra at Training and Inference Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16648)
*Montgomery Bohde,Hongxuan Liu,Mrunali Manjrekar,Magdalena Lederbauer,Shuiwang Ji,Runzhong Wang,Connor W. Coley*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FRIGID是一个基于扩散语言模型的框架，通过中间指纹表示和确定化学式，利用质谱条件生成分子结构，并通过推理时缩放显著提升准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决质谱解析中从头结构解析的挑战，利用大规模未标记结构数据训练模型，并通过推理时优化提升准确率。

Method: 提出基于扩散语言模型的FRIGID框架，通过中间指纹表示和化学式条件生成分子结构；引入前向碎片化模型实现推理时缩放，识别谱图不一致片段并通过定向重掩码和去噪进行优化。

Result: 在MassSpecGym基准测试中达到超过18%的Top-1准确率，在NPLIB1上使领先方法的Top-1准确率提升三倍；展示了对数线性性能缩放特性。

Conclusion: FRIGID为从头结构解析开辟了有前景的新方向，通过推理时计算扩展实现持续改进，代码已公开。

Abstract: In this work, we present FRIGID, a framework with a novel diffusion language model that generates molecular structures conditioned on mass spectra via intermediate fingerprint representations and determined chemical formulae, training at the scale of hundreds of millions of unlabeled structures. We then demonstrate how forward fragmentation models enable inference-time scaling by identifying spectrum-inconsistent fragments and refining them through targeted remasking and denoising. While FRIGID already achieves strong performance with its diffusion base, inference-time scaling significantly improves its accuracy, surpassing 18% Top-1 accuracy on the challenging MassSpecGym benchmark and tripling the Top-1 accuracy of the leading methods on NPLIB1. Further empirical analyses show that FRIGID exhibits log-linear performance scaling with increasing inference-time compute, opening a promising new direction for continued improvements in de novo structural elucidation. FRIGID code is publicly available at https://github.com/coleygroup/FRIGID

</details>


### [91] [UniCon: Unified Framework for Efficient Contrastive Alignment via Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16678)
*Hangke Sui,Yuqing Wang,Minh N Do*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: UniCon提出了一种基于核方法的统一框架，用于高效对比对齐，通过闭式全局解替代传统的随机优化，显著提升训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于对比目标的多模态模型训练速度慢，依赖长时间的随机优化，需要更高效的训练方法。

Method: 提出UniCon框架，引入对比相似度权重矩阵S(γ)，通过再生核希尔伯特空间理论提供核化视角，实现闭式全局解替代小批量反向传播。

Result: 在合成、单模态、多模态和零样本任务上的实验表明，UniCon在保持通用性和强经验性能的同时，实现了显著的效率提升。

Conclusion: UniCon为对比对齐提供了一个统一的核化框架，通过理论分析和实验验证展示了其高效性和通用性，揭示了对比对齐与谱方法之间的联系。

Abstract: Contrastive objectives power state-of-the-art multimodal models, but their training remains slow, relying on long stochastic optimization. We propose a Unified Framework for Efficient Contrastive Alignment via Kernels (UniCon), which spans linear and nonlinear encoders as well as one-to-one and many-to-many alignments. At its core, UniCon introduces the contrastive similarity weight matrix $S(γ)$, which enables closed-form global solutions that provably replace minibatch back-propagation with exact updates. Through the lens of reproducing kernel Hilbert spaces (RKHS), UniCon provides a kernelized perspective that unifies contrastive alignment and reveals its connection to spectral methods. To validate the theory, we conduct experiments on synthetic, unimodal, multimodal, and zero-shot tasks, demonstrating that UniCon achieves substantial efficiency gains while preserving generality and strong empirical performance.

</details>


### [92] [DARLING: Detection Augmented Reinforcement Learning with Non-Stationary Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16684)
*Argyrios Gerogiannis,Yu-Han Huang,Venugopal V. Veeravalli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DARLING是一种用于非平稳分段平稳MDP的模型无关强化学习算法，无需先验知识即可处理奖励和状态转移的动态变化，在表格和线性MDP中均能实现近乎最优的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究非平稳有限时域MDP中的模型无关强化学习问题，其中奖励和状态转移动态可以任意次数变化。现有方法在动态遗憾边界和实际性能方面仍有改进空间，需要开发无需变化先验知识的通用算法。

Method: 提出DARLING（Detection Augmented Reinforcement Learning）算法，这是一种模块化包装器，适用于分段平稳RL设置。算法包含变化点检测机制，无需知道变化发生的时间或次数，适用于表格和线性MDP两种场景。

Result: 在满足变化点分离和可达性条件下，DARLING改进了两种设置下的最佳可用动态遗憾边界，并展现出强大的实证性能。同时建立了表格和线性MDP中PS-RL的首个极小极大下界，证明DARLING是首个近乎最优的算法。

Conclusion: DARLING在标准基准测试中持续超越现有最先进方法，在多样化的非平稳场景下表现优异，为解决非平稳RL问题提供了有效的通用解决方案。

Abstract: We study model-free reinforcement learning (RL) in non-stationary finite-horizon episodic Markov decision processes (MDPs) without prior knowledge of the non-stationarity. We focus on the piecewise-stationary (PS) setting, where both the reward and transition dynamics can change an arbitrary number of times. We propose Detection Augmented Reinforcement Learning (DARLING), a modular wrapper for PS-RL that applies to both tabular and linear MDPs, without knowledge of the changes. Under certain change-point separation and reachability conditions, DARLING improves the best available dynamic regret bounds in both settings and yields strong empirical performance. We further establish the first minimax lower bounds for PS-RL in tabular and linear MDPs, showing that DARLING is the first nearly optimal algorithm. Experiments on standard benchmarks demonstrate that DARLING consistently surpasses the state-of-the-art methods across diverse non-stationary scenarios.

</details>


### [93] [Graph Transformer-Based Pathway Embedding for Cancer Prognosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16685)
*Koushik Howlader,Md Tauhidul Islam,Wei Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PATH：一种基于调制的患者条件基因嵌入策略，通过共享基因基础嵌入和患者特异性CNV/突变信号动态适应，结合图变换器进行通路交互建模，在泛癌转移预测中实现SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 癌症进展预测面临分子组学数据高异质性挑战。现有方法在基因编码构建通路表示方面存在局限：分层模型直接映射原始分子输入，集成框架依赖简单的患者信号统计聚合，未能显式学习每个基因的共享基础表示，限制了通路嵌入的表达能力和生物学准确性。

Method: 提出PATH策略：1）为每个基因建立共享基础嵌入，保持跨人群的稳定生物学身份；2）使用患者特异性CNV和突变信号动态调整基础嵌入；3）将PATH集成到图变换器框架中，通过通路引导注意力建模生物连接通路间的交互。

Result: 在泛癌转移预测中，PATH达到F1分数0.8766，比当前SOTA多组学基准提升8.8%。除了预测准确性优势外，方法能识别生物学意义通路，并揭示疾病状态特异性通路重连，为癌症进展中的通路-通路交互演变提供新见解。

Conclusion: PATH通过共享基因基础嵌入和患者特异性调制，实现了更准确和生物学可解释的癌症进展预测。该方法不仅提升了预测性能，还能揭示疾病状态特异性通路重连，为理解癌症进展机制提供了新视角。

Abstract: Accurate prediction of cancer progression remains a challenge due to the high heterogeneity of molecular omics data across patients. While biologically informed models have improved the interpretability of these predictions, a persistent limitation lies in how they encode individual genes to construct pathway representations. Existing hierarchical models typically derive gene features by directly mapping raw molecular inputs, whereas integration frameworks often rely on simple statistical aggregations of patient-level signals. These approaches often fail to explicitly learn a shared base representation for each gene, thereby limiting the expressiveness and biological accuracy of downstream pathway embeddings. To address this, we introduce PATH, a modulation-based, patient-conditioned gene embedding strategy. PATH represents a paradigm shift by starting from a shared base embedding for each gene, preserving a stable biological identity across the population, and then dynamically adapting it using patient-specific copy number variation (CNV) and mutation signals. This allows the model to capture subtle individual molecular variations while maintaining a consistent latent understanding of the gene itself. We integrate PATH into a graph transformer framework that models interactions among biologically connected pathways through pathway-guided attention. Across pancancer metastasis prediction, PATH achieves an F1 score of 0.8766, representing an 8.8 percent improvement over the current SOTA multi-omics benchmarks. Beyond superior predictive accuracy, our approach identifies biologically meaningful pathways and, crucially, reveals disease-state-specific pathway rewiring, offering new insights into the evolving pathway-pathway interactions that drive cancer progression.

</details>


### [94] [Chronax: A Jax Library for Univariate Statistical Forecasting and Conformal Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16719)
*Xan Carey,Yash Deshmukh,Aileen Huang,Sunit Jadhav,Omkar Tekawade,Lorraine Yang,Anvesha Tiwary,Gerardo Riano,Amy Greenwald,Denizalp Goktas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Chronax是一个基于JAX的原生时间序列预测库，通过函数式编程范式重新设计预测系统，解决了传统Python数值栈在可组合性、大规模并行化和现代可微分工作流集成方面的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前时间序列预测软件生态系统仍停留在传统的Python数值栈，依赖于解释器驱动执行和面向对象抽象，限制了可组合性、大规模并行化以及与现代化可微分和加速器工作流的集成。同时，现代预测任务涉及大规模异构时间序列数据、不规则协变量和频繁重训练，对可扩展性和执行效率提出了新要求。

Method: Chronax采用JAX的函数式编程范式，将预处理、建模和多步预测表示为纯JAX函数，利用即时编译和自动向量化等程序转换技术，实现端到端的CPU、GPU和TPU优化。

Result: Chronax实现了可扩展的多序列预测、模型无关的符合性不确定性量化，并能无缝集成到现代机器学习和科学计算流程中。

Conclusion: Chronax通过将JAX的函数式编程范式引入时间序列预测系统设计，解决了传统预测库在可组合性、并行化和现代工作流集成方面的局限性，为大规模异构时间序列预测提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Time-series forecasting is central to many scientific and industrial domains, such as energy systems, climate modeling, finance, and retail. While forecasting methods have evolved from classical statistical models to automated, and neural approaches, the surrounding software ecosystem remains anchored to the traditional Python numerical stack. Existing libraries rely on interpreter-driven execution and object-oriented abstractions, limiting composability, large-scale parallelism, and integration with modern differentiable and accelerator-oriented workflows. Meanwhile, today's forecasting increasingly involves large collections of heterogeneous time series data, irregular covariates, and frequent retraining, placing new demands on scalability and execution efficiency. JAX offers an alternative paradigm to traditional stateful numerical computation frameworks based on pure functions and program transformations such as just-in-time compilation and automatic vectorization, enabling end-to-end optimization across CPUs, GPUs, and TPUs. However, this modern paradigm has not yet been fully incorporated into the design of forecasting systems. We introduce Chronax, a JAX-native time-series forecasting library that rethinks forecasting abstractions around functional purity, composable transformations, and accelerator-ready execution. By representing preprocessing, modeling, and multi-horizon prediction as pure JAX functions, Chronax enables scalable multi-series forecasting, model-agnostic conformal uncertainty quantification, and seamless integration with modern machine learning and scientific computing pipelines.

</details>


### [95] [Late Fusion Neural Operators for Extrapolation Across Parameter Space in Partial Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16721)
*Eva van Tegelen,Taniya Kapoor,George A. K. van Voorn,Peter van Heijster,Ioannis N. Athanasiadis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Late Fusion Neural Operator架构，通过解耦状态动力学和参数效应学习，在四个PDE基准测试中显著优于现有方法，域内和域外RMSE平均降低约72%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在科学和工程应用中，开发能够准确预测偏微分方程系统行为的神经算子至关重要。实际应用中物理参数的变化会导致训练和预测机制之间的分布偏移，使得外推成为核心挑战。参数如何融入神经算子模型对其泛化能力起着关键作用，特别是当状态和参数表示纠缠时。

Method: 提出Late Fusion Neural Operator架构，将学习状态动力学与参数效应解耦。该方法结合神经算子学习潜在状态表示，并使用稀疏回归以结构化方式融入参数信息。

Result: 在四个基准PDE（包括平流、Burgers以及1D和2D反应扩散方程）测试中，该方法始终优于Fourier Neural Operator和CAPE-FNO。Late Fusion Neural Operators在所有实验中均取得最佳性能，与次优方法相比，域内RMSE平均降低72.9%，域外降低71.8%。

Conclusion: 结果表明该方法在域内和域外参数机制中都具有强大的泛化能力，通过解耦状态动力学和参数效应学习，显著提高了神经算子的预测性能。

Abstract: Developing neural operators that accurately predict the behavior of systems governed by partial differential equations (PDEs) across unseen parameter regimes is crucial for robust generalization in scientific and engineering applications. In practical applications, variations in physical parameters induce distribution shifts between training and prediction regimes, making extrapolation a central challenge. As a result, the way parameters are incorporated into neural operator models plays a key role in their ability to generalize, particularly when state and parameter representations are entangled. In this work, we introduce the Late Fusion Neural Operator, an architecture that disentangles learning state dynamics from parameter effects, improving predictive performance both within and beyond the training distribution. Our approach combines neural operators for learning latent state representations with sparse regression to incorporate parameter information in a structured manner. Across four benchmark PDEs including advection, Burgers, and both 1D and 2D reaction-diffusion equations, the proposed method consistently outperforms Fourier Neural Operator and CAPE-FNO. Late Fusion Neural Operators achieve consistently the best performance in all experiments, with an average RMSE reduction of 72.9% in-domain and 71.8% out-domain compared to the second-best method. These results demonstrate strong generalization across both in-domain and out-domain parameter regimes.

</details>


### [96] [LLM-Extracted Covariates for Clinical Causal Inference: Rethinking Integration Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16763)
*Lei Liu,Jialin Chen,Kathy Macropol*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LLM提取的文本特征能显著改善电子健康记录中的因果推断，通过增强倾向评分模型效果最佳，而双重卡钳匹配方法效果较差


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子健康记录中的因果推断受限于未测量的混杂因素，关键临床状态如衰弱、护理目标和精神状态通常记录在自由文本中但未结构化。大型语言模型可以提取这些潜在混杂因素作为可解释的结构化协变量，但如何有效将其整合到因果估计流程中尚未系统研究。

Method: 使用MIMIC-IV数据库中21,859名脓毒症患者，比较了七种协变量整合策略，包括仅表格基线、传统NLP表示和三种LLM增强方法。在已知真实效果的半合成实验中评估性能，并在真实数据上应用双重稳健估计器验证结果。

Result: LLM增强的倾向评分模型表现最佳，将估计偏差从0.0143降低到0.0003；在真实数据中，纳入LLM提取的协变量将估计治疗效果从0.055降低到0.027，与CLOVERS随机试验方向一致；双重稳健估计器得到0.019的结果证实了这一发现的稳健性。

Conclusion: 直接增强倾向评分模型是整合文本衍生协变量的最有效策略，而基于文本衍生分类距离的双重卡钳匹配会限制供体池并降低估计质量。研究结果为在重症监护中何时以及如何使用文本衍生协变量改善因果估计提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Causal inference from electronic health records (EHR) is fundamentally limited by unmeasured confounding: critical clinical states such as frailty, goals of care, and mental status are documented in free-text notes but absent from structured data. Large language models can extract these latent confounders as interpretable, structured covariates, yet how to effectively integrate them into causal estimation pipelines has not been systematically studied. Using the MIMIC-IV database with 21,859 sepsis patients, we compare seven covariate-integration strategies for estimating the effect of early vasopressor initiation on 28-day mortality, spanning tabular-only baselines, traditional NLP representations, and three LLM-augmented approaches. A central finding is that not all integration strategies are equally effective: directly augmenting the propensity score model with LLM covariates achieves the best performance, while dual-caliper matching on text-derived categorical distances restricts the donor pool and degrades estimation. In semi-synthetic experiments with known ground-truth effects, LLM-augmented propensity scores reduce estimation bias from 0.0143 to 0.0003 relative to tabular-only methods, and this advantage persists under substantial simulated extraction error. On real data, incorporating LLM-extracted covariates reduces the estimated treatment effect from 0.055 to 0.027, directionally consistent with the CLOVERS randomized trial, and a doubly robust estimator yielding 0.019 confirms the robustness of this finding. Our results offer practical guidance on when and how text-derived covariates improve causal estimation in critical care.

</details>


### [97] [Representation Before Training: A Fixed-Budget Benchmark for Generative Medical Event Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16775)
*Inhyeok Lee,Luke Solo,Michael C. Burkhart,Bashar Ramadan,William F. Parker,Brett K. Beaulieu-Jones*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究评估了医疗事件生成模型中输入表示方法对下游预测性能的影响，通过三个实验比较了不同的量化粒度、参考范围锚定、编码-值融合、值编码和时间编码策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式医疗事件模型的预测性能受临床事件标记化方式的限制，但输入表示通常与其他系统和架构选择混杂在一起，需要独立评估表示决策对下游预测的影响。

Method: 在MIMIC-IV数据集上训练28个匹配的transformer模型，通过三个实验评估30个临床结果：1)量化粒度、参考范围锚定和编码-值融合；2)值编码（硬分箱、软离散化、编码归一化xVal）与时间编码（事件顺序、时间标记、入院相对RoPE）的交叉；3)原生MIMIC实验室/生命体征编码与CLIF重映射编码的比较。

Result: 编码-值融合标记化将死亡率AUROC从0.891提升到0.915，住院时长AUROC从0.763提升到0.788；事件顺序和入院相对RoPE编码在缩短序列11%的同时性能相当或优于时间标记；CLIF重映射在保持性能的同时提供了更小、临床可解释的标记集。

Conclusion: 输入表示决策显著影响医疗事件模型的预测性能，编码-值融合、高效的时间编码和标准化的临床编码重映射是提升模型效果的重要策略，为多站点医疗AI应用提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Every prediction from a generative medical event model is bounded by how clinical events are tokenized, yet input representation is rarely isolated from other system and architectural choices. We evaluate how representation decisions affect downstream prediction after a shared one-epoch pretraining budget. We train 28 matched transformers on MIMIC-IV and evaluate them on 30 clinical outcomes in three experiments: (1) quantization granularity, reference-range anchoring, and code-value fusion; (2) value encoding (hard bins, soft discretization, code-normalized xVal) crossed with temporal encoding (event order, time tokens, admission-relative RoPE); and (3) native MIMIC laboratory/vital codes versus the Common Longitudinal ICU Format (CLIF)-remapped laboratory/vital codes with compression-preserving perturbation arms. In Experiment 1, fused code-value tokenization improves mortality AUROC from 0.891 to 0.915 (BH-adjusted p < 0.001), hospital length-of-stay AUROC from 0.763 to 0.788 (BH-adjusted p < 0.001), and, for the decile fused-vs-unfused comparison, mean regression Spearman rho across the 13 regression outcomes from 0.414 to 0.494. Across the three temporal encodings, event order only and admission-relative RoPE match or exceed inserting time tokens on average while shortening sequences by 11%. CLIF remapping preserves downstream performance in our single-site setting while yielding a smaller, clinically interpretable token set compatible with multi-site use. Finer-than-decile quantization, reference-range anchoring, and soft discretization help in selective outcomes, while code-normalized xVal remains well below the discrete and soft families, consistent with near-median suppression that persists after the affine variant.

</details>


### [98] [Continuous Limits of Coupled Flows in Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16801)
*Zilin Li,Weiwei Xu,Xuchun Tong,Xuanbo Lu,Xuanqi Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文建立了一个理论框架，将去中心化学习形式化为黎曼流形上的耦合慢-快动力系统，证明了离散空间转移收敛到过阻尼Langevin随机微分方程，并建立了全局耗散性和特征正交解耦的理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代表示学习严重依赖全局误差信号，而去中心化算法提供了一种分布式替代方案。然而，这些离散动力学在连续数据流形上的宏观收敛特性在理论上仍未解决，且存在参数爆炸问题。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 1. 将去中心化学习形式化为黎曼流形上的耦合慢-快动力系统；2. 使用测度理论极限证明离散空间转移一致收敛到过阻尼Langevin随机微分方程；3. 通过Itô-Poisson预解式和LaSalle不变原理的随机扩展，建立表示权重无条件避免发散并与空间测度的主特征空间严格对齐；4. 为完全耦合的空间-参数流构造联合Lyapunov泛函。

Result: 1. 证明了离散空间转移收敛到过阻尼Langevin SDE；2. 表示权重无条件避免发散并与空间测度的主特征空间严格对齐；3. 全局耗散性得到证明；4. 在平稳极限处，正交解耦、线性可分的特征自发出现。

Conclusion: 该框架将离散算法与连续随机分析连接起来，为去中心化表示学习提供了正式的理论基准，证明了正交解耦特征的自发出现和全局收敛性。

Abstract: While modern representation learning relies heavily on global error signals, decentralized algorithms driven by local interactions offer a fundamental distributed alternative. However, the macroscopic convergence properties of these discrete dynamics on continuous data manifolds remain theoretically unresolved, notoriously suffering from parameter explosion. We bridge this gap by formalizing decentralized learning as a coupled slow-fast dynamical system on Riemannian manifolds. First, using measure-theoretic limits, we prove that the discrete spatial transitions converge uniformly to an overdamped Langevin stochastic differential equation. Second, via the Itô-Poisson resolvent and a stochastic extension of LaSalle's Invariance Principle, we establish that the representation weights unconditionally avoid divergence and align strictly with the principal eigenspace of the spatial measure. Finally, we construct a joint Lyapunov functional for the fully coupled spatial-parametric flow. This proves global dissipativity and demonstrates that orthogonally disentangled, linearly separable features emerge spontaneously at the stationary limit. Our framework bridges discrete algorithms with continuous stochastic analysis, providing a formal theoretical baseline for decentralized representation learning.

</details>


### [99] [AutoOR: Scalably Post-training LLMs to Autoformalize Operations Research Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16804)
*Sumeet Ramesh Motwani,Chuan Du,Aleksander Petrov,Christopher Davis,Philip Torr,Antonio Papania-Davis,Weishi Yan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AutoOR是一个基于合成数据生成和强化学习的管道，用于训练LLM将自然语言描述的优化问题自动形式化为可求解的数学表达式，在多个OR基准测试中达到最先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业决策中的优化问题需要专业运筹学知识才能转化为可求解的数学形式，这限制了规模化应用。需要开发能够自动形式化自然语言描述的优化问题的AI系统。

Method: AutoOR采用合成数据生成和强化学习管道：1）从标准优化形式生成经过验证的训练数据；2）使用求解器执行反馈作为强化学习的奖励信号；3）针对非线性物理动力学问题，引入课程学习策略从有限初始数据引导训练。

Result: 在六个已建立的OR基准测试中，AutoOR应用于8B模型取得了最先进或具有竞争力的结果，匹配了显著更大的前沿模型。对于前沿模型得分接近0%的非线性物理动力学问题类，AutoOR通过课程学习策略使其变得可处理。

Conclusion: AutoOR等方法可以显著加速工业决策的AI应用，通过自动形式化优化问题，降低对专业运筹学知识的依赖，提高可扩展性。

Abstract: Optimization problems are central to decision-making in manufacturing, logistics, scheduling, and other industrial settings. Translating complicated descriptions of these problems into solver-ready formulations requires specialized operations research (OR) expertise, making it hard to scale. We present AutoOR, a scalable synthetic data generation and reinforcement learning pipeline that trains LLMs to autoformalize optimization problems specified in natural language across linear, mixed-integer, and non-linear categories. AutoOR generates verified training data from standard optimization forms and uses solver execution feedback as the reward signal for RL post-training. AutoOR applied to an 8B model achieves state-of-the-art or competitive results across six established OR benchmarks, matching significantly larger frontier models. For a non-linear problem class involving physical dynamics, where frontier models score near 0%, we introduce a curriculum RL strategy that bootstraps from limited initial training data to make this class tractable for post-training. We believe that methods such as AutoOR can significantly accelerate industrial decision-making with AI.

</details>


### [100] [Self-Reinforcing Controllable Synthesis of Rare Relational Data via Bayesian Calibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16817)
*Chongsheng Zhang,Hao Wang,Zelong Yu,Esteban Garces Arias,Julian Rodemann,Zhanshuo Zhang,Qilong Li,Gaojuan Fan,Krikamol Muandet,Christian Heumann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RDDG是一个用于不平衡关系数据生成的统一上下文学习框架，通过渐进式思维链步骤生成表格数据，提升下游不平衡分类性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实应用中普遍存在不平衡数据问题，虽然数据合成能有效缓解稀有类别的数据稀缺问题，但LLMs在关系/结构化表格数据合成中的应用仍未被充分探索，且现有方法缺乏有效的反馈机制来指导LLMs在合成过程中持续优化生成数据质量。

Method: RDDG采用统一的上下文学习框架，包含：1）核心集选择从原始数据中识别代表性样本；2）上下文学习发现核心集中属性间的内在模式和相关性；3）在保持上述约束条件下生成表格数据；4）最重要的是包含自我强化反馈机制，对生成数据质量进行自动评估，实现生成过程中的持续质量优化。

Result: 在多个真实和合成数据集上的实验结果表明，RDDG在数据保真度和下游不平衡分类性能方面均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: RDDG通过动态指导机制有效解决了不平衡关系数据生成问题，为LLMs在表格数据合成中的应用提供了新思路，并通过开源代码促进相关研究发展。

Abstract: Imbalanced data is commonly present in real-world applications. While data synthesis can effectively mitigate the data scarcity problem of rare-classes, and LLMs have revolutionized text generation, the application of LLMs to relational/structured tabular data synthesis remains underexplored. Moreover, existing approaches lack an effective feedback mechanism that can guide LLMs towards continuously optimizing the quality of the generated data throughout the synthesis process. In this work, we propose RDDG, Relational Data generator with Dynamic Guidance, which is a unified in-context learning framework that employs progressive chain-of-thought (CoT) steps to generate tabular data for enhancing downstream imbalanced classification performance. RDDG first uses core set selection to identify representative samples from the original data, then utilizes in-context learning to discover the inherent patterns and correlations among attributes within the core set, and subsequently generates tabular data while preserving the aforementioned constraints. More importantly, it incorporates a self-reinforcing feedback mechanism that provides automatic assessments on the quality of the generated data, enabling continuous quality optimization throughout the generation process. Experimental results on multiple real and synthetic datasets demonstrate that RDDG outperforms existing approaches in both data fidelity and downstream imbalanced classification performance. We make our code available at https://github.com/cszhangLMU/RDDG.

</details>


### [101] [R&F-Inventory: A Large-Scale Dataset for Monotonic Inventory Estimation in Reach and Frequency Advertising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16821)
*Yunshan Peng,Ji Wu,Wentao Bai,Yunke Bai,Jinan Pang,Wenzheng Shu,Yanxiang Zeng,Xialong Liu,Peng Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出并发布了一个大规模R&F合约库存估计数据集，该数据集以"定向-排期-频控"为基本上下文，提供同一上下文中多个预算点对应的UV和PV观测值，形成完整的预算-性能曲线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公开广告数据集大多基于独立样本，缺乏对R&F合约中"预算-性能曲线"核心结构的刻画。广告主在创建R&F合约时需要实时查看不同预算水平下的UV、PV变化曲线，但现有数据无法满足这一需求。

Method: 构建以R&F合约上下文（定向-排期-频控）为基础的大规模数据集，提供同一上下文中多个预算点对应的UV和PV观测值，形成完整预算-性能曲线。数据集包含基于时间窗口的频控机制，并满足预算和排期维度的单调性和边际收益递减特性。

Result: 数据集可用于系统性研究结构约束学习、单调回归、曲线一致性建模和R&F合约规划等问题。论文定义了两个标准化基准任务：单点性能预测和预算-性能曲线重建，并提供可复现的基线方法和评估协议。

Conclusion: 该数据集填补了R&F合约广告领域的数据空白，支持对预算-性能曲线结构的研究，为广告库存估计和合约规划提供了重要的数据基础。

Abstract: Reach and Frequency (R&F) contract advertising is an important form of widely used brand advertising. Unlike performance advertising, R&F contracts emphasize controllable delivery of UV and PV under given targeting, scheduling, and frequency control constraints. In practical systems, advertisers typically need to view the UV, PV change curves at different budget levels in real time when creating an R&F contract. However, most existing publicly available advertising datasets are based on independent samples, lacking a characterization of the core structure of the "budget-performance curve" (including UV and PV) in R&F contracts.This paper proposes and releases a large-scale R&F contract inventory estimation dataset. This dataset uses the R&F contract context consisting of "targeting-scheduling-frequency control" as the basic context, providing observations of UV and PV corresponding to multiple budget points within the same context, thus forming a complete budget-performance curve. The dataset explicitly includes a time-window-based frequency control mechanism (e.g.,"no more than 3 times within 5 days") and naturally satisfies the monotonicity and diminishing marginal returns characteristics in the budget and scheduling dimensions. We further derive the theoretical maximum exposure ceiling and use it as a consistency check to evaluate data quality and the feasibility of model predictions. Using this data set, this paper defines two standardized benchmark tasks: single-point performance prediction and reconstruction of budget-performance curves, and provides a set of reproducible baseline methods and evaluation protocols. This dataset can support systematic research on problems such as structural constraint learning, monotonic regression, curve consistency modeling, and R&F contract planning.The code for our experiments can be found at https://github.com/pengyunshan/RF-Inventory.

</details>


### [102] [The Illusion of Certainty: Decoupling Capability and Calibration in On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16830)
*Jiaxin Zhang,Xiangyu Peng,Qinglin Chen,Qinyuan Ye,Caiming Xiong,Chien-Sheng Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OPD提升任务精度但导致严重过度自信，作者提出CaOPD框架通过学生模型经验估计置信度，实现帕累托最优校准


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发现在线蒸馏存在普遍的错误校准缩放定律：虽然有效提升任务精度，但系统性地导致模型过度自信。这源于信息不匹配问题——教师监督基于训练时的特权上下文，而部署模型只能使用部署时信息报告置信度。

Method: 提出校准感知的在线蒸馏框架CaOPD：1）从模型rollouts估计经验置信度；2）用学生基础目标替换自报告置信度；3）通过相同的自蒸馏流程蒸馏修正后的响应。

Result: 在各种模型和领域上的实验表明，CaOPD实现了帕累托最优校准，同时保持竞争力，在分布外和持续学习场景下具有鲁棒泛化能力。

Conclusion: 能力蒸馏并不意味着校准置信度，置信度应被视为后训练中的重要目标。CaOPD框架为解决在线蒸馏中的校准问题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) is an increasingly important paradigm for post-training language models. However, we identify a pervasive Scaling Law of Miscalibration: while OPD effectively improves task accuracy, it systematically traps models in severe overconfidence. We trace this failure to an information mismatch: teacher supervision is formed under privileged context available during training, whereas the deployed model must report confidence using only deployment-time information. We formalize this perspective theoretically, showing that teacher-conditioned success is generally not a valid target for deployment-time confidence and that helpful privileged context induces entropy collapse and a systematic optimism bias. To address this, we propose a calibration-aware OPD framework, CaOPD, that estimates empirical confidence from model rollouts, replaces self-reported confidence with this student-grounded target, and distills the revised response through the same self-distillation pipeline. Experiments across various models and domains show that CaOPD achieves Pareto-optimal calibration while maintaining competitive capability, generalizing robustly under out-of-distribution and continual learning. Our findings highlight that capability distillation does not imply calibrated confidence, and that confidence should be treated as an essential objective in post-training. Code: https://github.com/SalesforceAIResearch/CaOPD

</details>


### [103] [CCAR: Intrinsic Robustness as an Emergent Geometric Property](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16861)
*Akash Samanta,Manish Pratap Singh,Debasis Chaudhuri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CCAR方法通过类条件激活正则化在特征空间中施加块对角结构，将不同类别的能量限制在正交子空间中，从而创建几何支架来过滤噪声和对抗扰动，提升模型鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统监督学习只关注预测准确性，忽略了学习特征的内部几何结构，导致表示纠缠且脆弱。需要显式地设计特征空间几何结构来提升模型的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出类条件激活正则化(CCAR)，通过软归纳偏置在特征空间中施加块对角结构，将不同类别的能量限制在正交子空间中，创建几何支架来自然过滤噪声和对抗扰动。

Result: 理论分析表明该方法与Fisher判别比最大化相关，建立了几何解缠与算法稳定性之间的形式化联系。在标签噪声和输入损坏基准测试中显著优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 鲁棒性是良好设计的特征空间的涌现属性。通过显式地工程化特征空间几何结构，可以创建内在的几何支架来提升模型对噪声和对抗扰动的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Standard supervised learning optimizes for predictive accuracy but remains agnostic to the internal geometry of learned features, often yielding representations that are entangled and brittle. We propose Class-Conditional Activation Regularization (CCAR) to explicitly engineer the feature space, imposing a block-diagonal structure via a soft inductive bias. By shaping the latent representation to confine class energy to orthogonal subspaces, we create an intrinsic geometric scaffold that naturally filters noise and adversarial perturbations. We provide theoretical analysis linking this structural constraint to the maximization of the Fisher Discriminant Ratio, establishing a formal connection between geometric disentanglement and algorithmic stability. Empirically, this approach demonstrates that robustness is an emergent property of a well-engineered feature space, significantly outperforming baselines on label noise and input corruption benchmarks.

</details>


### [104] [OC-Distill: Ontology-aware Contrastive Learning with Cross-Modal Distillation for ICU Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16878)
*Zhongyuan Liang,Junhyung Jo,Hyang-Jung Lee,Sang Kyu Kim,Irene Y. Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: OC-Distill是一个两阶段框架，利用多模态监督训练但仅需生命体征进行推理，通过本体感知对比学习和跨模态知识蒸馏提升ICU临床预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期预测严重临床恶化和剩余住院时间对于ICU等高风险环境至关重要，但现有方法存在局限性：对比预训练将所有患者视为同等负样本，忽略了临床相关诊断间的相似性；下游微调通常忽略临床记录等补充模态，而这些模态提供了生理信号无法提供的丰富上下文信息。

Method: 提出OC-Distill两阶段框架：第一阶段引入本体感知对比目标，利用ICD层次结构量化患者相似性并学习临床基础表示；第二阶段通过跨模态知识蒸馏微调预训练编码器，将临床记录中的补充信息转移到模型中。训练时使用多模态监督，但推理时仅需生命体征。

Result: 在MIMIC数据集上的多个ICU预测任务中，OC-Distill展示了改进的标签效率，并在仅使用生命体征进行推理的方法中实现了最先进的性能。

Conclusion: OC-Distill通过结合本体感知对比学习和跨模态知识蒸馏，有效解决了现有方法的局限性，为ICU临床预测提供了更准确、更高效的解决方案，同时保持了推理时的实用性（仅需生命体征）。

Abstract: Early prediction of severe clinical deterioration and remaining length of stay can enable timely intervention and better resource allocation in high-acuity settings such as the ICU. This has driven the development of machine learning models that leverage continuous streams of vital signs and other physiological signals for real-time risk prediction. Despite their promise, existing methods have important limitations. Contrastive pretraining treats all patients as equally strong negatives, failing to capture clinically meaningful similarity between patients with related diagnoses. Meanwhile, downstream fine-tuning typically ignores complementary modalities such as clinical notes, which provide rich contextual information unavailable in physiological signals alone. To address these challenges, we propose OC-Distill, a two-stage framework that leverages multimodal supervision during training while requiring only vital signs at inference. In the first stage, we introduce an ontology-aware contrastive objective that exploits the ICD hierarchy to quantify patient similarity and learn clinically grounded representations. In the second stage, we fine-tune the pretrained encoder via cross-modal knowledge distillation, transferring complementary information from clinical notes into the model. Across multiple ICU prediction tasks on MIMIC, OC-Distill demonstrates improved label efficiency and achieves state-of-the-art performance among methods that use only vital signs at inference.

</details>


### [105] [SinkRouter: Sink-Aware Routing for Efficient Long-Context Decoding in Large Language and Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16883)
*Junnan Liu,Xinyan Liu,Peifeng Gao,Zhaobo Qi,Beichen Zhang,Weigang Zhang,Antoni Bert Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SinkRouter：一种基于注意力汇点固定点理论的训练无关选择性路由框架，通过检测汇点信号跳过近零输出计算，在保持精度的同时显著提升长上下文解码效率，在512K上下文下达到2.03倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长上下文解码加速方法存在两个主要问题：1）依赖启发式剪枝，可能丢弃有用信息，以效率换取精度；2）对注意力汇点现象机制理解不足，倾向于盲目保留高分token、将早期token视为必需锚点或依赖启发式头路由。

Method: 提出SinkRouter框架：1）理论层面揭示注意力汇点现象对应训练中构建的稳定、可达、误差可控的固定点；2）基于此设计训练无关的选择性路由框架，检测汇点信号并跳过产生近零输出的计算；3）开发硬件感知的Triton内核，采用块级分支和Split-K并行化实现实际加速。

Result: 在多样化长上下文基准测试（LongBench、InfiniteBench、CVBench、MileBench、MMVP）上，使用文本和多模态骨干模型（Llama-3.1-8B/70B、Yi-9B-200K、LLaVA-1.5-7B/13B）进行广泛评估，SinkRouter在保持竞争性精度的同时持续提升解码效率，在512K上下文下达到2.03倍加速。

Conclusion: 通过深入理解注意力汇点的固定点特性，SinkRouter提供了一种机制驱动的加速方法，避免了启发式方法的精度-效率权衡，为长上下文解码提供了高效且准确的解决方案。

Abstract: In long-context decoding for LLMs and LMMs, attention becomes increasingly memory-bound because each decoding step must load a large amount of KV-cache data from GPU memory. Existing acceleration strategies often trade efficiency for accuracy by relying on heuristic pruning that may discard useful information. At a deeper level, they also tend to indiscriminately preserve all high-scoring tokens, treat early tokens as indispensable anchors, or rely on heuristic head routing, reflecting an insufficient mechanistic understanding of the attention sink phenomenon. In this paper, we show that the attention sink phenomenon corresponds to a stable, reachable, and error-controllable fixed point constructed during training. Based on this insight, we propose SinkRouter, a training-free selective routing framework that detects the sink signal and skips computations that would otherwise produce near-zero output. To translate this mechanism into real-world acceleration, we develop a hardware-aware Triton kernel with block-level branching and Split-K parallelism. We conduct extensive evaluations on a diverse suite of long-context benchmarks, including LongBench, InfiniteBench, CVBench, MileBench, and MMVP, using both text-only and multimodal backbones such as Llama-3.1-8B, Llama-3.1-70B, Yi-9B-200K, LLaVA-1.5-7B, and LLaVA-1.5-13B. Across these settings, SinkRouter consistently improves decoding efficiency while maintaining competitive accuracy, and reaches 2.03x speedup with a 512K context.

</details>


### [106] [Towards Fully Parameter-Free Stochastic Optimization: Grid Search with Self-Bounding Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16888)
*Yuheng Zhao,Yu-Hu Yan,Amit Attia,Tomer Koren,Lijun Zhang,Peng Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个名为Grasp的通用网格搜索框架，通过自边界分析技术实现完全参数自由的优化方法，无需依赖问题参数的先验知识。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数自由优化方法虽然不依赖具体参数值，但仍需要参数的上下界等先验知识，属于"部分参数自由"。本文旨在实现"完全参数自由"的方法，即算法输入不需要满足任何与真实问题参数相关的不可验证条件。

Method: 提出Grasp网格搜索框架，采用新颖的自边界分析技术有效确定参数的搜索范围。该方法具有通用性：在非凸情况下实现完全参数自由方法；在凸情况下实现具有加速和通用性的参数自由方法。

Result: 在非凸情况下，提出的完全参数自由方法达到接近最优的收敛速率（对数因子内）；在凸情况下，参数自由方法在加速和通用性方面表现强劲。此外，在插值方差表征下，为网格搜索框架的最后一步——模型集成提供了更尖锐的保证。

Conclusion: Grasp框架通过自边界分析技术实现了真正完全参数自由的优化方法，在非凸和凸优化问题中都表现出色，为参数自由优化领域提供了通用且强大的解决方案。

Abstract: Parameter-free stochastic optimization aims to design algorithms that are agnostic to the underlying problem parameters while still achieving convergence rates competitive with optimally tuned methods. While some parameter-free methods do not require the specific values of the problem parameters, they still rely on prior knowledge, such as the lower or upper bounds of them. We refer to such methods as ``partially parameter-free''. In this work, we target achieving ``fully parameter-free'' methods, i.e., the algorithmic inputs do not need to satisfy any unverifiable condition related to the true problem parameters. We propose a powerful and general grid search framework, named \textsc{Grasp}, with a novel self-bounding analysis technique that effectively determines the search ranges of parameters, in contrast to previous work. Our method demonstrates generality in: (i) the non-convex case, where we propose a fully parameter-free method that achieves near-optimal convergence rate, up to logarithmic factors; (ii) the convex case, where our parameter-free methods are competitive with strong performance in terms of acceleration and universality. Finally, we contribute a sharper guarantee for the model ensemble, a final step of the grid search framework, under interpolated variance characterization.

</details>


### [107] [Covariance-Based Structural Equation Modeling in Small-Sample Settings with $p>n$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16894)
*Hiroki Hasegawa,Aoba Tamura,Yukihiko Okada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种新的因子结构方程模型估计方法，解决小样本（p>n）情况下传统似然估计失效的问题，通过重构协方差结构实现稳定估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于似然的结构方程模型估计需要非奇异样本协方差矩阵，但在小样本设置（p>n）下会失效，限制了模型在实际应用中的可用性。

Method: 将协方差结构重新表述为自协方差和交叉协方差分量，建立基于似然的可行集结合相对误差约束的框架，实现在p>n情况下的稳定估计。

Result: 在合成和真实数据实验中显示出改进的稳定性，特别是在恢复结构参数的符号和方向方面表现优异。

Conclusion: 该方法将基于协方差的结构方程模型扩展到小样本设置，为决策制定提供了实用的方向性信息。

Abstract: Factor-based Structural Equation Modeling (SEM) relies on likelihood-based estimation assuming a nonsingular sample covariance matrix, which breaks down in small-sample settings with $p>n$. To address this, we propose a novel estimation principle that reformulates the covariance structure into self-covariance and cross-covariance components. The resulting framework defines a likelihood-based feasible set combined with a relative error constraint, enabling stable estimation in small-sample settings where $p>n$ for sign and direction. Experiments on synthetic and real-world data show improved stability, particularly in recovering the sign and direction of structural parameters. These results extend covariance-based SEM to small-sample settings and provide practically useful directional information for decision-making.

</details>


### [108] [Noise-Adaptive Diffusion Sampling for Inverse Problems Without Task-Specific Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16919)
*Yingzhi Xia,Setthakorn Tanomkiattikun,Liangli Zhen,Zaiwang Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为噪声空间哈密顿蒙特卡洛（N-HMC）的后验采样方法，通过将反向扩散视为从初始噪声到干净图像的确定性映射，解决了逆问题中的局部最优和噪声过拟合问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在逆问题上表现出色，但现有方法存在局限性：基于优化的方法容易陷入局部最优和噪声过拟合；贝叶斯方法在去噪过程中强制测量一致性会导致流形不可行问题。

Method: 提出N-HMC方法，将推理完全转移到初始噪声空间，将反向扩散视为确定性映射，从而全面探索解空间并保持提案在学习的数据流形上。还扩展了噪声自适应变体（NA-NHMC）来处理未知噪声类型和水平的逆问题。

Result: 在四个线性和三个非线性逆问题上的广泛实验表明，NA-NHMC实现了卓越的重建质量，在不同超参数和初始化下具有鲁棒性能，显著优于最近的最先进方法。

Conclusion: N-HMC通过将推理转移到噪声空间，有效解决了逆问题中的局部最优和流形不可行问题，其自适应变体NA-NHMC能够处理未知噪声情况，为逆问题提供了强大的后验采样框架。

Abstract: Diffusion models (DMs) have recently shown remarkable performance on inverse problems (IPs). Optimization-based methods can fast solve IPs using DMs as powerful regularizers, but they are susceptible to local minima and noise overfitting. Although DMs can provide strong priors for Bayesian approaches, enforcing measurement consistency during the denoising process leads to manifold infeasibility issues. We propose Noise-space Hamiltonian Monte Carlo (N-HMC), a posterior sampling method that treats reverse diffusion as a deterministic mapping from initial noise to clean images. N-HMC enables comprehensive exploration of the solution space, avoiding local optima. By moving inference entirely into the initial-noise space, N-HMC keeps proposals on the learned data manifold. We provide a comprehensive theoretical analysis of our approach and extend the framework to a noise-adaptive variant (NA-NHMC) that effectively handles IPs with unknown noise type and level. Extensive experiments across four linear and three nonlinear inverse problems demonstrate that NA-NHMC achieves superior reconstruction quality with robust performance across different hyperparameters and initializations, significantly outperforming recent state-of-the-art methods. The code is available at https://github.com/NA-HMC/NA-HMC.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [109] [Semantic Consensus: Process-Aware Conflict Detection and Resolution for Enterprise Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16339)
*Vivek Acharya*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对多智能体LLM系统在企业环境中高达86.7%的失败率问题，提出了语义共识框架（SCF），通过六个组件解决智能体间的语义意图分歧，实现了100%的工作流完成率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业AI自动化中多智能体LLM系统失败率高达41%-86.7%，其中79%的失败源于规范和协调问题而非模型能力限制。语义意图分歧——由于孤立的上下文和缺乏过程模型导致合作LLM智能体对共享目标产生不一致解释——是主要但未正式解决的根本原因。

Method: 提出语义共识框架（SCF），包含六个组件：过程上下文层（共享操作语义）、语义意图图（形式化意图表示）、冲突检测引擎（实时识别矛盾、竞争和因果无效的意图组合）、共识解决协议（使用策略-权威-时间层次结构）、漂移监控器（检测渐进语义分歧）和过程感知治理集成层（组织策略执行）。

Result: 在三个多智能体框架（AutoGen、CrewAI、LangGraph）和四个企业场景的600次运行评估中，SCF是唯一实现100%工作流完成率的方法（相比次优基线的25.1%），检测到65.2%的语义冲突（精度27.9%），并提供完整的治理审计跟踪。框架与MCP和A2A通信标准兼容。

Conclusion: 语义共识框架（SCF）通过解决多智能体LLM系统中的语义意图分歧问题，显著提高了企业AI自动化的可靠性和成功率，为多智能体协调提供了系统化的解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-agent large language model (LLM) systems are rapidly emerging as the dominant architecture for enterprise AI automation, yet production deployments exhibit failure rates between 41% and 86.7%, with nearly 79% of failures originating from specification and coordination issues rather than model capability limitations. This paper identifies Semantic Intent Divergence--the phenomenon whereby cooperating LLM agents develop inconsistent interpretations of shared objectives due to siloed context and absent process models--as a primary yet formally unaddressed root cause of multi-agent failure in enterprise settings. We propose the Semantic Consensus Framework (SCF), a process-aware middleware comprising six components: a Process Context Layer for shared operational semantics, a Semantic Intent Graph for formal intent representation, a Conflict Detection Engine for real-time identification of contradictory, contention-based, and causally invalid intent combinations, a Consensus Resolution Protocol using a policy--authority--temporal hierarchy, a Drift Monitor for detecting gradual semantic divergence, and a Process-Aware Governance Integration layer for organizational policy enforcement. Evaluation across 600 runs spanning three multi-agent frameworks (AutoGen, CrewAI, LangGraph) and four enterprise scenarios demonstrates that SCF is the only approach to achieve 100% workflow completion--compared to 25.1% for the next-best baseline--while detecting 65.2% of semantic conflicts with 27.9% precision and providing complete governance audit trails. The framework is protocol-agnostic and compatible with MCP and A2A communication standards.

</details>


### [110] [Computational Hermeneutics: Evaluating generative AI as a cultural technology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16403)
*Cody Kommers,Ruth Ahnert,Maria Antoniak,Emmanouil Benetos,Steve Benford,Mercedes Bunz,Baptiste Caramiaux,Shauna Concannon,Martin Disley,James Dobson,Yali Du,Edgar Duéñez-Guzmán,Kerry Francksen,Evelyn Gius,Jonathan W. Y. Gray,Ryan Heuser,Sarah Immel,Richard Jean So,Sang Leigh,Dalaki Livingston,Hoyt Long,Meredith Martin,Georgia Meyer,Daniela Mihai,Ashley Noel-Hirst,Kirsten Ostherr,Deven Parker,Yipeng Qin,Jessica Ratcliff,Emily Robinson,Karina Rodriguez,Adam Sobey,Ted Underwood,Aditya Vashistha,Matthew Wilkens,Youyou Wu,Yuan Zheng,Drew Hemment*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出生成式AI应被视为"语境机器"，需从诠释学角度评估其文化理解能力，而非仅测量准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI评估框架常将文化视为可测量变量，而非系统运作的根本要素。生成式AI作为文化技术，需要新的评估范式来理解其在文化语境中的运作。

Method: 借鉴人文学科的诠释学理论，提出生成式AI是"语境机器"，面临三个诠释挑战：情境性（意义只在语境中产生）、多元性（多种有效解释共存）、模糊性（解释自然冲突）。提出计算诠释学作为评估框架。

Result: 提出三个诠释学评估原则：基准测试应是迭代而非一次性；应包含人而不仅是机器；应测量文化语境而不仅是模型输出。这为AI系统设计和评估提供了新范式。

Conclusion: 计算诠释学为评估当代AI系统提供了新兴框架，将评估焦点从标准化的准确性问题转向语境化的意义问题，为AI作为文化技术的理解提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Generative AI systems are increasingly recognized as cultural technologies, yet current evaluation frameworks often treat culture as a variable to be measured rather than fundamental to the system's operation. Drawing on hermeneutic theory from the humanities, we argue that GenAI systems function as "context machines" that must inherently address three interpretive challenges: situatedness (meaning only emerges in context), plurality (multiple valid interpretations coexist), and ambiguity (interpretations naturally conflict). We present computational hermeneutics as an emerging framework offering an interpretive account of what GenAI systems do, and how they might do it better. We offer three principles for hermeneutic evaluation -- that benchmarks should be iterative, not one-off; include people, not just machines; and measure cultural context, not just model output. This perspective offers a nascent paradigm for designing and evaluating contemporary AI systems: shifting from standardized questions about accuracy to contextual ones about meaning.

</details>


### [111] [Heterogeneous Self-Play for Realistic Highway Traffic Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16406)
*Jinkai Qiu,Alessandro Saviolo,Chaojie Wang,Mingke Wang,Xiaoyu Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PHASE是一个用于高速公路自动驾驶安全评估的异构智能体自博弈框架，通过上下文感知、可控场景生成和闭环多智能体训练，实现真实的高速公路交通模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高速公路自动驾驶的安全评估需要大规模的真实模拟，但现有方法难以同时满足：1）覆盖不同速度和机动动作的广泛场景；2）可控生成罕见的安全关键场景；3）多智能体交互的行为可信度。

Method: PHASE采用上下文感知的自博弈框架，包括：显式的智能体条件控制、合成场景生成、闭环多智能体训练；支持不同车辆类型（轿车、拖挂卡车）；通过车辆感知动力学、上下文条件动作、早期终止、碰撞责任归属、高速公路奖励塑造、耦合课程学习和鲁棒策略优化来稳定训练。

Result: 在exiD数据集512个未见真实场景上实现零样本迁移：成功率96.3%，ADE/FDE从6.57/12.07米降至2.44/5.25米；在轨迹嵌入空间中，相比IDM模型，Fréchet轨迹距离减少13.1%，能量距离减少20.2%。

Conclusion: 合成自博弈方法无需直接模仿专家数据，就能为高速公路场景生成提供可扩展、可控且真实的途径，有效支持自动驾驶的安全评估。

Abstract: Realistic highway simulation is critical for scalable safety evaluation of autonomous vehicles, particularly for interactions that are too rare to study from logged data alone. Yet highway traffic generation remains challenging because it requires broad coverage across speeds and maneuvers, controllable generation of rare safety-critical scenarios, and behavioral credibility in multi-agent interactions. We present PHASE, Policy for Heterogeneous Agent Self-play on Expressway, a context-aware self-play framework that addresses these three requirements through explicit per-agent conditioning for controllability, synthetic scenario generation for broad highway coverage, and closed-loop multi-agent training for realistic interaction dynamics. PHASE further supports different vehicle profiles, for example, passenger cars and articulated trailer trucks, within a single policy via vehicle-aware dynamics and context-conditioned actions, and stabilizes self-play with early termination of unrecoverable states, at-fault collision attribution, highway-aware reward shaping, coupled curricula, and robust policy optimization. Despite being trained only on synthetic data, PHASE transfers zero-shot to 512 unseen high-interaction real scenarios in exiD, achieving a 96.3% success rate and reducing ADE/FDE from 6.57/12.07 m to 2.44/5.25 m relative to a prior self-play baseline. In a learned trajectory embedding space, it also improves behavioral realism over IDM, reducing Frechet trajectory distance by 13.1% and energy distance by 20.2%. These results show that synthetic self-play can provide a scalable route to controllable and realistic highway scenario generation without direct imitation of expert logs.

</details>


### [112] [Support Sufficiency as Consequence-Sensitive Compression in Belief Arbitration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16434)
*Mark Walsh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出支持感知控制状态的概念，认为系统压缩假设证据结构时，需要保留足够的支持信息来指导下游控制决策，而不是简单地选择内容和标量置信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法假设选择的内容和标量置信度足以支持下游控制，但本文认为这种压缩会丢失关键的证据结构信息，导致验证、弃权和恢复等控制决策出现错误。

Method: 开发了循环仲裁架构，通过活动约束场共同确定候选假设的几何结构，并将该几何结构压缩为支持感知的控制状态，其分辨率由当前后果几何、仲裁记忆和资源约束共同调节。

Result: 模拟实验显示：自适应控制器在累积效用上优于所有固定分辨率控制器；敏捷自适应控制优于迟钝自适应控制；固定高分辨率控制虽然承诺准确性最好，但由于资源成本和学习碎片化问题，仍落后于自适应控制器。

Conclusion: 支持充分性不应被视为静态表示阈值，而应是动态压缩标准。稳健的仲裁取决于在当前后果环境下保留足够的最小支持结构，并在推理和行动循环中随着条件变化调节该结构。

Abstract: When a system commits to a hypothesis, much of the evidential structure behind that commitment is lost to compression. Standard accounts assume that selected content and scalar confidence suffice for downstream control. This paper argues that they do not, and that determining what must survive compression is itself a consequence-sensitive problem. We develop a recurrent arbitration architecture in which active constraint fields jointly determine a hypothesis geometry over candidates. Rather than carrying that geometry forward in full, the system compresses it into a support-aware control state whose resolution is regulated by current consequence geometry, arbitration memory, and resource constraints.
  A bounded objective formalizes the tradeoff. Too little retained support collapses policy-relevant distinctions, producing controllers that select content adequately while misrouting verification, abstention, and recovery. Too much retained support fragments learning across overly fine contexts, degrading adaptation even as discrimination improves. These failure modes yield ordered controller predictions confirmed by a minimal repeated-interaction simulation. Adaptive controllers that regulate support resolution outperform all fixed-resolution controllers in cumulative utility. Agile adaptive control outperforms sluggish adaptive control. Fixed high-resolution control achieves the best commitment accuracy but still trails adaptive controllers because resource cost and learning fragmentation offset the gains from richer retention.
  Support sufficiency should be understood not as a static representational threshold, but as a dynamic compression criterion. Robust arbitration depends on preserving the smallest support structure adequate for policy under the current consequence landscape, and on regulating that structure as conditions change across repeated cycles of inference and action.

</details>


### [113] [Healthcare AI for Automation or Allocation? A Transaction Cost Economics Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16465)
*Ari Ercole*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究使用任务级数据分析医疗保健工作中的协调摩擦，发现临床角色的交易成本强度显著高于非临床角色，主要源于信息搜索和决策相关的协调负担，这表明数字和AI干预的机会分布不均，更多受协调结构而非技术任务复杂性影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗保健生产力不仅受临床复杂性影响，还受不确定性下协调工作的成本影响。交易成本经济学提供了协调摩擦的理论框架，但很少在医疗职业的任务层面进行操作化研究。

Method: 使用O*NET职业数据库中的任务陈述和频率权重，在任务粒度上表征医疗保健工作；使用受限的大型语言模型将每个独特任务编码到主导的交易成本类别（信息搜索、决策和谈判、监督和执行、或适应和协调），并计算总体交易成本强度得分；在职业层面进行聚合分析。

Result: 临床角色的交易成本强度显著高于非临床角色，主要驱动因素是更大的信息搜索和决策相关协调负担；职业内部的交易成本分散度没有差异。

Conclusion: 研究发现医疗保健角色间协调工作性质存在系统性异质性，数字和AI干预的机会分布不均，更多受底层协调结构而非技术任务复杂性的影响。

Abstract: Healthcare productivity is shaped not only by clinical complexity but by the costs of coordinating work under uncertainty. Transaction-cost economics offers a theory of these coordination frictions, yet has rarely been operationalised at task level across health occupations. Using task statements and frequency weights from the O*NET occupational database, we characterised healthcare work at task granularity and coded each unique task using a constrained large language model into one dominant transaction-cost category (information search, decision and bargaining, monitoring and enforcement, or adaptation and coordination) together with an overall transaction-cost intensity score.
